                assert!(!held.has(boon), "{boon:?} was offered though it is held");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_drained_pool_has_only_the_wall_swap_left() {
    // What stops boons swallowing the weapon roster: the pool drains. It never
    // empties completely, because the two walls are one setting and the one not
    // in use is always a live choice - but one option out of eleven is not what
    // swallows a roster.
    let v = Viewport::new(1280.0, 800.0);
    let mut rng = Rng::new(4242);
    let all = Boons {
        double_jump: true,
        dash_free: true,
        shield: true,
        wall: WallMod::Push,
    };
    let mut weapons = 0;
    let mut boons = 0;
    for _ in 0..200 {
        let offer = Offer::roll(&v, 0.0, 700.0, AttackKind::Hammer, 2, all, 1, WaveRule::Normal, 0, &mut rng);
        for choice in offer.choices.iter() {
            match choice.item {
                OfferItem::Attack { .. } => weapons += 1,
                OfferItem::Boon(b) => {
                    assert_eq!(b, Boon::WallPull, "a spent boon came back round");
                    boons += 1;
                }
                OfferItem::Rule(r) => panic!("an early wave offered terms: {r:?}"),
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(weapons > boons * 3, "a drained pool should be mostly weapons");
}

#[test]
fn every_boon_can_come_up() {
    let v = Viewport::new(1280.0, 800.0);
    let mut rng = Rng::new(777);
    let mut seen = [false; Boon::ALL.len()];
    let mut saw_weapon = false;
    for _ in 0..2000 {
        let offer = Offer::roll(
            &v,
            0.0,
            700.0,
            AttackKind::Basic,
            1,
            Boons::default(),
            1,
            WaveRule::Normal,
            0,
            &mut rng,
        );
        for choice in offer.choices.iter() {
            match choice.item {
                OfferItem::Boon(b) => {
                    seen[Boon::ALL.iter().position(|x| *x == b).unwrap()] = true
                }
                OfferItem::Attack { .. } => saw_weapon = true,
                OfferItem::Rule(r) => panic!("an early wave offered terms: {r:?}"),
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(seen.iter().all(|s| *s), "some boon can never be drawn");
    assert!(saw_weapon, "weapons should still share the pool");
}

/* ---------------- the modified wall ---------------- */

/// A run with `wall`, one enemy planted `offset` from the player, and a charge
/// ready to spend.
fn wall_and_one_enemy(wall: WallMod, offset: f32) -> Game {
    let mut game = player_with(Boons { wall, ..Default::default() });
    let v = game.viewport;
    game.state = State::Playing;
    game.players[0].super_charges = 1;
    let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    z.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + offset;
    z.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - z.body.h;
    z.ax = 0.0;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    game.zombies.push(z);
    game
}

/// One tick of jumping, which is what the slam needs to be airborne.
fn leave_the_floor(game: &mut Game) {
    let mut frame = idle();
    frame.players[0].jump = true;
    game.tick(&frame);
}

/// One tick of the slam button, which is what raises the wall.
///
/// Note this tick also runs the enemies, so the first step of the shove is
/// spent inside it - a measurement taken afterwards has already missed a fifth
/// of the distance.
fn slam(game: &mut Game) {
    let mut frame = idle();
    frame.players[0].slam = true;
    game.tick(&frame);
}

/// Raises the wall the way the game does - in the air, with the slam button.
fn slam_up_the_wall(game: &mut Game) {
    leave_the_floor(game);
    slam(game);
}

/// How far the one enemy on the field is moved over `ticks`, counting from the
/// tick the wall goes up.
///
/// The shove is written into the enemy's own velocity, so there is no moment it
/// stops: it bleeds off. What can be pinned down is the distance over a window,
/// which is what [`WALL_SHOVE_AX_PCT`] is aimed at.
fn distance_moved_by_the_wall(game: &mut Game, flyer: bool, ticks: i32) -> f32 {
    let read = |g: &Game| if flyer { g.flyers[0].body.x } else { g.zombies[0].body.x };
    leave_the_floor(game);
    let before = read(game);
    slam(game);
    for _ in 1..ticks {
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    read(game) - before
}

#[test]
fn a_plain_wall_leaves_the_field_where_it_stands() {
    // Not "ax is zero": an enemy walking toward the player has a speed of its
    // own, and it was never the wall that gave it one.
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Plain, 400.0);
    let want = game.viewport.wper(10.0);
    let travelled = distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, false, WALL_SHOVE_TICKS).abs();
    assert!(
        travelled < want / 4.0,
        "an unmodified wall moved the field {travelled}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_black_wall_drags_the_field_in() {
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Pull, 400.0);
    let want = game.viewport.wper(10.0);
    let travelled = -distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, false, WALL_SHOVE_TICKS);
    assert!(
        (travelled - want).abs() < want * 0.02,
        "it should have come {want} closer, not {travelled}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_grey_wall_clears_the_field_out() {
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Push, 400.0);
    let want = game.viewport.wper(10.0);
    let travelled = distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, false, WALL_SHOVE_TICKS);
    assert!(
        (travelled - want).abs() < want * 0.02,
        "it should have been thrown {want} clear, not {travelled}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_field_is_moved_toward_or_away_whichever_side_it_is_on() {
    // Read off the side the enemy stands on, not off which way it happens to
    // be walking - otherwise a wall would gather half the field and scatter
    // the rest.
    for offset in [400.0, -400.0] {
        let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Pull, offset);
        let gap = |g: &Game| (g.zombies[0].body.x - g.players[0].body.x).abs();
        let before = gap(&game);
        slam_up_the_wall(&mut game);
        for _ in 0..WALL_SHOVE_TICKS {
            game.tick(&idle());
        }
        assert!(gap(&game) < before, "an enemy at {offset} was not drawn in");
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_hold_is_one_shove_and_not_a_standing_force() {
    // A lasting pull is a tractor beam and a lasting push makes the wall
    // unapproachable. Neither is a wall.
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Push, 400.0);
    slam_up_the_wall(&mut game);
    for _ in 0..60 {
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    let after = game.zombies[0].body.x;
    for _ in 0..20 {
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    assert!(
        game.zombies[0].body.x < after,
        "it should have gone back to walking in"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_leaper_mid_leap_is_moved_like_everything_else() {
    // Its trajectory takes an early exit out of the chase; the shove has to be
    // checked before that or the one enemy hardest to catch would ignore it.
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Push, 300.0);
    let v = game.viewport;
    game.zombies[0].movement = Movement::Leap(Leap { crouch: 0, airborne: true });
    game.zombies[0].ax = -v.wper(2.0);
    game.zombies[0].body.y -= v.hper(20.0);
    let before = game.zombies[0].body.x;

    slam_up_the_wall(&mut game);
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(
        game.zombies[0].body.x > before,
        "a leaper flew through the wall's hold untouched"
    );
}

#[test]
fn flyers_are_field_too() {
    let mut game = player_with(Boons { wall: WallMod::Push, ..Default::default() });
    let v = game.viewport;
    game.state = State::Playing;
    game.players[0].super_charges = 1;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    let size = game.players[0].body;
    let mut f = Flyer::from_edge(&v, &size, 0, &mut game.rng);
    f.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + 300.0;
    game.flyers.push(f);
    let want = v.wper(10.0);
    let travelled = distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, true, WALL_SHOVE_TICKS);
    assert!(
        (travelled - want).abs() < want * 0.02,
        "a flyer should be thrown {want} clear, not {travelled}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_shove_coasts_rather_than_stopping_dead() {
    // Living in the enemy's own velocity means there is no tick it ends on. The
    // window the constant is aimed at is the first five; after that it keeps
    // going, more slowly, for about another fifteen.
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Push, 400.0);
    let v = game.viewport;
    let five = distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, false, WALL_SHOVE_TICKS);
    let mut game = wall_and_one_enemy(WallMod::Push, 400.0);
    let twenty = distance_moved_by_the_wall(&mut game, false, 20);
    assert!(
        twenty > five * 2.0,
        "it should still be travelling after the window: {five} then {twenty}"
    );
    assert!(
        twenty < v.wper(30.0),
        "but a third of the view would be a launch, not a shove"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_shoved_flyer_goes_back_to_cruising() {
    // Nothing in a flyer's own update ever lowers its speed, so without the
    // bleed-off the shove would become the speed it keeps for good.
    let mut game = player_with(Boons { wall: WallMod::Push, ..Default::default() });
    let v = game.viewport;
    game.state = State::Playing;
    game.players[0].super_charges = 1;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    let size = game.players[0].body;
    let mut f = Flyer::from_edge(&v, &size, 0, &mut game.rng);
    f.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + 300.0;
    game.flyers.push(f);

    slam_up_the_wall(&mut game);
    assert!(game.flyers[0].ax.abs() > v.wper(FLYER_CRUISE_PCT));
    for _ in 0..60 {
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    assert!(
        game.flyers[0].ax.abs() <= v.wper(FLYER_CRUISE_PCT) + 0.01,
        "a shoved flyer kept the speed for good: {}",
        game.flyers[0].ax
    );
}

#[test]
fn nothing_moving_at_its_own_pace_is_touched_by_the_bleed_off() {
    // The deceleration only ever applies above cruising speed, so an ordinary
    // flyer crosses the screen exactly as it always did.
    let mut game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    game.state = State::Playing;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    let size = Body::new(0.0, 0.0, v.wper(5.0), v.hper(10.0));
    game.flyers.push(Flyer::from_edge(&v, &size, 0, &mut game.rng));
    let cruise = game.flyers[0].ax;
    for _ in 0..30 {
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    assert_eq!(game.flyers[0].ax, cruise, "cruising speed should be left alone");
}

#[test]
fn the_two_walls_replace_each_other() {
    // A wall cannot pull and push at once, so they are one setting. That also
    // means the offer keeps showing the other one, which is the swap.
    let mut boons = Boons::default();
    boons.take(Boon::WallPull);
    assert_eq!(boons.wall, WallMod::Pull);
    assert!(boons.has(Boon::WallPull) && !boons.has(Boon::WallPush));

    boons.take(Boon::WallPush);
    assert_eq!(boons.wall, WallMod::Push);
    assert!(boons.has(Boon::WallPush) && !boons.has(Boon::WallPull));
}

#[test]
fn holding_one_wall_leaves_the_other_on_the_table() {
    let v = Viewport::new(1280.0, 800.0);
    let mut rng = Rng::new(9001);
    let held = Boons {
        double_jump: true,
        dash_free: true,
        shield: true,
        wall: WallMod::Pull,
    };
    let mut saw_push = false;
    for _ in 0..500 {
        let offer = Offer::roll(&v, 0.0, 700.0, AttackKind::Basic, 1, held, 1, WaveRule::Normal, 0, &mut rng);
        for choice in offer.choices.iter() {
            if let OfferItem::Boon(b) = choice.item {
                assert_ne!(b, Boon::WallPull, "the wall it already has was offered");
                saw_push = true;
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(saw_push, "the other wall should still come up as a swap");
}

#[test]
fn each_wall_is_a_flat_slab_of_its_own_shade() {
    // Three shades far enough apart to be told apart at a glance, and nothing
    // else: no outline, so the colour carries the whole warning.
    let shades = [WallMod::Plain, WallMod::Pull, WallMod::Push].map(|w| w.color().r);
    assert!(shades[0] > 200.0, "the plain wall should be white");
    assert!(shades[1] < 32.0, "the pulling wall should be black");
    assert!(shades[2] > 100.0 && shades[2] < 200.0, "the pushing wall should be grey");
    for pair in [(0, 1), (1, 2), (0, 2)] {
        let gap = (shades[pair.0] - shades[pair.1]).abs();
        assert!(gap > 90.0, "two walls are only {gap} apart");
    }
}

/* ---------------- the offer between waves ---------------- */

/// Ticks until the standing options accept a hit.
fn wait_for_armed(game: &mut Game) {
    for _ in 0..OFFER_ARM_TICKS + 4 {
        if game.offer.as_ref().is_some_and(|o| o.armed(game.timer)) {
            return;
        }
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    panic!("the offer never went live");
}

/// Runs a wave out so the lull - and any offer with it - begins.
fn into_the_lull(game: &mut Game) {
    for _ in 0..8000 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.waves.between_waves() {
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!("the wave never cleared");
}

#[test]
fn an_offer_arrives_once_the_team_has_earned_it() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "nothing is owed at the start");

    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    let offer = game.offer.as_ref().expect("the lull should be offering");
    assert_eq!(offer.choices.len(), 3);
}

#[test]
fn the_three_options_are_all_different() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);

    let c = &game.offer.as_ref().unwrap().choices;
    assert!(c[0].item != c[1].item && c[1].item != c[2].item && c[0].item != c[2].item);
    for choice in c.iter() {
        assert_ne!(
            choice.item,
            OfferItem::Attack { kind: AttackKind::Basic, level: 1 },
            "the plain attack is not an upgrade"
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn an_offer_ignores_the_swing_that_ended_the_wave() {
    // The wave ends while the player is still swinging at the last of it, and
    // the options appear inside that swing. Found in play: every option was
    // being taken by accident.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);

    let offer = game.offer.as_ref().expect("the lull should be offering");
    assert!(!offer.armed(game.timer), "it must not be live the moment it lands");
    let target = offer.choices[0].body;

    // Swinging at one for the whole delay changes nothing.
    let mut swings = 0;
    while !game.offer.as_ref().is_some_and(|o| o.armed(game.timer)) {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
        game.tick(&idle());
        swings += 1;
        assert!(
            game.offer.is_some(),
            "an option was taken after {swings} swings, before the offer went live"
        );
    }
    assert!(swings > 60, "the pause was only {swings} ticks - too short to help");
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack, AttackKind::Basic, "nothing was taken");

    // And once it is live, the same swing takes it.
    assert!(game.offer.as_ref().unwrap().armed(game.timer));
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "it should be takeable now");
}

#[test]
fn hitting_an_option_arms_every_player() {
    // One offer, shared: either player may take it and both end up carrying it.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(2);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);

    let taken = game.offer.as_ref().unwrap().choices[1];
    wait_for_armed(&mut game);
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, taken.body);
    game.tick(&idle());

    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "taking one clears the rest");
    for p in game.players.iter() {
        match taken.item {
            OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } => {
                assert_eq!(p.attack, kind, "both players should be carrying it");
                assert_eq!(p.attack_level, level);
            }
            OfferItem::Boon(boon) => {
                assert!(p.boons.has(boon), "both players should have been given it");
            }
            OfferItem::Rule(r) => panic!("an early wave offered terms: {r:?}"),
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn an_offer_can_be_declined_by_starting_the_wave() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_some());

    game.waves.skip_countdown();
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "the lull ending takes it off the table");
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack, AttackKind::Basic, "and nothing was taken");
}

#[test]
fn the_view_is_pinned_while_the_choice_is_up() {
    // The options stand in the view; walking off would lose them.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);

    let camera = game.camera_x;
    let mut walk = idle();
    walk.players[0].right = true;
    for _ in 0..120 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&walk);
    }
    assert_eq!(game.camera_x, camera, "the view should not have moved");
}

#[test]
fn a_second_offer_costs_another_full_step() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);

    let first = game.offer.as_ref().unwrap().choices[0];
    wait_for_armed(&mut game);
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, first.body);
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(game.offer.is_none());

    // Same score, next lull: nothing more is owed.
    game.waves.skip_countdown();
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "one score should not buy two");

    game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP * 2;
    game.waves.skip_countdown();
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_some(), "another step should buy another");
}

#[test]
fn the_options_are_rolled_from_their_own_generator() {
    // Which three turn up must not shift which waves and variants follow, the
    // same reason the soundtrack has a generator of its own.
    let wave_stream = |offered: bool| {
        let mut game = new_game();
        game.start_run(1);
        settle_on_floor(&mut game);
        if offered {
            game.players[0].score = OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
        }
        into_the_lull(&mut game);
        (game.waves.kind, game.waves.rule)
    };
    assert_eq!(
        wave_stream(false),
        wave_stream(true),
        "rolling an offer changed the wave that followed"
    );
}

/* ---------------- the combo finisher ---------------- */

/// Kills one enemy with a swing and reports the blasts left behind.
///
/// The bystander test below does not go through a swing at all: a full combo
/// doubles the attack box to 13% of the view while the blast only reaches 10%
/// out from where it started, so there is no spot that is inside one and
/// outside the other. The two have to be checked apart.
fn blast_damage_after_kill(combo: u32) -> f32 {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    clear_arena(&mut game);

    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut victim = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    victim.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - victim.body.h;
    victim.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + v.wper(6.0);
    victim.hp = 1.0;
    let victim_body = victim.body;
    game.zombies.push(victim);
    game.flyers.clear();

    game.players[0].combo = combo;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, victim_body);
    // The death check runs before the damage does, so the kill - and the blast
    // with it - lands on the tick after the one that took the last of its
    // health.
    for _ in 0..3 {
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
    }

    game.explosions
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.damage)
        .fold(0.0f32, f32::max)
}

#[test]
fn a_combo_kill_leaves_a_blast_that_bites() {
    // What keeping the rhythm is worth - and what every upgrade trades away,
    // since only the plain attack makes one.
    assert_eq!(blast_damage_after_kill(2), COMBO_BLAST_DAMAGE);
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_kill_leaves_a_blast_that_does_not() {
    assert_eq!(blast_damage_after_kill(0), 0.0, "a plain death left a live blast");
}

#[test]
fn a_live_blast_bites_once_however_long_it_covers_you() {
    // It grows over a hundred ticks; damage on each of them would make the
    // finisher worth more than a wall. Placed by hand rather than earned, so
    // the swing that would have made it cannot also reach the target.
    const MARK: f32 = 777_777.0;
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    clear_arena(&mut game);

    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut target = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    target.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - target.body.h;
    target.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + v.wper(40.0);
    target.hp = MARK;
    target.hpmax = MARK;
    target.speed_multiplier = 0.0;
    let at = target.body;
    game.zombies.push(target);
    game.flyers.clear();

    game.explosions.push(Explosion::lethal(
        at.x,
        at.y,
        &v,
        COMBO_BLAST_DAMAGE,
        4242,
        0,
    ));

    for _ in 0..120 {
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    let left = game
        .zombies
        .iter()
        .find(|z| z.hpmax == MARK)
        .expect("the target wandered off")
        .hp;
    assert_eq!(MARK - left, COMBO_BLAST_DAMAGE, "exactly one bite");
}

#[test]
fn the_generator_is_usable_in_its_low_bits() {
    // Everything that picks between a handful of things takes a remainder, and
    // a remainder reads the lowest bits. A raw xorshift's lowest bits follow a
    // recurrence short enough to see: `range(0, 3)` once went four hundred
    // draws in a live run without ever returning 0, which took one wave rule
    // out of five out of the game.
    let mut rng = Rng::new(1);
    let mut counts = [0usize; 4];
    const DRAWS: usize = 40_000;
    for _ in 0..DRAWS {
        counts[rng.range(0, 3) as usize] += 1;
    }
    let want = DRAWS / 4;
    for (value, seen) in counts.iter().enumerate() {
        let off = (*seen as i64 - want as i64).abs();
        assert!(
            off < want as i64 / 10,
            "range(0, 3) returned {value} {seen} times in {DRAWS}, wanted about {want}"
        );
    }

    // The single lowest bit, which is what `flip` reads.
    let mut rng = Rng::new(1);
    let heads = (0..DRAWS).filter(|_| rng.flip()).count();
    let off = (heads as i64 - want as i64 * 2).abs();
    assert!(off < DRAWS as i64 / 20, "flip came up heads {heads} times in {DRAWS}");
}

#[test]
fn every_wave_rule_still_reaches_a_real_run() {
    // The distribution above is the mechanism; this is the thing that broke.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    let mut seen = alloc::vec![false; 6];
    for _ in 0..400 {
        let was = game.wave;
        for _ in 0..200 {
            game.spawn_count = wave_budget(game.wave);
            game.zombies.clear();
            game.flyers.clear();
            game.tick(&idle());
            if game.wave > was {
                break;
            }
        }
        let i = match game.waves.rule {
            WaveRule::Normal => 0,
            WaveRule::StaticCamera => 1,
            WaveRule::NoJumps => 2,
            WaveRule::NoWall => 3,
            WaveRule::Hidden => 4,
            WaveRule::NoFooting => 5,
        };
        seen[i] = true;
    }
    assert!(seen.iter().all(|s| *s), "some rule never came up: {seen:?}");
}

/* ---------------- the rolled boss ---------------- */

/// Runs a boss wave and reports what it put out.
fn boss_wave(wave: i64, seed: u64) -> (alloc::vec::Vec<BossKind>, usize, bool) {
    let mut manager = WaveManager::default();
    let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
    manager.begin_wave(wave, &mut rng);
    manager.skip_countdown();
    let (mut spawned, mut plain, mut cleared) = (0i64, 0usize, false);
    let mut kinds = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..500_000 {
        match manager.update(wave, spawned, 0, &mut rng) {
            WaveAction::Idle => {}
            WaveAction::ClearWave => {
                cleared = true;
                break;
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnBosses(n) => {
                for _ in 0..n {
                    kinds.push(BossKind::Ground);
                }
                spawned += n;
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnFlyingBoss => {
                kinds.push(BossKind::Flying);
                spawned += 1;
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnShedderBoss => {
                kinds.push(BossKind::Shedder);
                spawned += 1;
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnRolledBoss => {
                kinds.push(BossKind::Rolled);
                spawned += 1;
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnBossGroup(k) => {
                spawned += k.len() as i64;
                kinds.extend(k);
            }
            WaveAction::SpawnElite(n) => spawned += n as i64,
            _ => {
                plain += 1;
                spawned += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    (kinds, plain, cleared)
}

#[test]
fn the_twentieth_wave_is_one_boss_and_nothing_else() {
    let (kinds, plain, cleared) = boss_wave(ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE, 4242);
    assert_eq!(kinds, alloc::vec![BossKind::Rolled]);
    assert_eq!(plain, 0, "the wave should spawn nothing but the boss");
    assert!(cleared, "and it still has to be able to end");
}

#[test]
fn a_wave_that_spawns_nothing_can_still_finish() {
    // The trap in switching ordinary spawning off: a wave ends when its budget
    // is spent, and a wave that never spawns never spends one. It would sit a
    // hundred and fifty short of a number it was never going to reach.
    let (_, _, cleared) = boss_wave(ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE, 7);
    assert!(cleared);
    // And the HUD must not count against that number either.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    game.wave = ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE;
    assert_eq!(game.wave_kill_target(), None);
    game.wave = ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE + 1;
    assert_eq!(game.wave_kill_target(), Some(wave_budget(ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE + 1)));
}

#[test]
fn the_rolled_boss_is_a_boss_by_every_measure_the_game_uses() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let recipe = Recipe {
        movement: MoveKind::Run,
        size: Size::Normal,
        shoot: false,
        blink: false,
        shed: true,
        brood: false,
    };
    let boss = recipe.build_boss(&v, ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE, 0, &mut game.rng);
    assert!(boss.is_boss, "the wall and the payout both read this flag");
    assert_eq!(boss.hpmax, boss_hp(ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE));
    assert_eq!(boss.hpmax, Zombie::boss(&v, ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE, &mut game.rng).hpmax);
    assert_eq!(boss.armor, 1.0, "boss health is the difficulty; armour on top is not");

    // Two traits are forced whatever the roll said.
    assert!(boss.broods, "making its own crowd is the whole fight");
    assert!(
        !boss.behaviors.shed,
        "a hazard left by something that takes hundreds of hits would fill the floor"
    );
}

#[test]
fn late_boss_waves_grow_and_mix() {
    for (wave, want) in [(25i64, 2usize), (30, 3), (35, 4), (40, 5)] {
        let (kinds, _, _) = boss_wave(wave, wave as u64 * 13 + 1);
        assert_eq!(kinds.len(), want, "wave {wave} carried the wrong number");
    }
    // Drawn separately, so a late wave is a mixed set rather than a row of the
    // same thing. Over enough runs every kind has to turn up.
    let mut seen = alloc::vec![false; BossKind::ALL.len()];
    for seed in 0..200u64 {
        for kind in boss_wave(40, seed).0 {
            seen[BossKind::ALL.iter().position(|k| *k == kind).unwrap()] = true;
        }
    }
    assert!(seen.iter().all(|s| *s), "some boss kind is never drawn: {seen:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_early_boss_waves_are_left_as_they_were() {
    assert_eq!(boss_wave(5, 1).0, alloc::vec![BossKind::Ground]);
    assert_eq!(boss_wave(FLYING_BOSS_WAVE, 1).0, alloc::vec![BossKind::Flying]);
    // Fifteen is a coin flip between three ground bosses and one shedder.
    let mut saw_both = (false, false);
    for seed in 0..40u64 {
        match boss_wave(SHEDDER_BOSS_WAVE, seed).0.len() {
            1 => saw_both.0 = true,
            3 => saw_both.1 = true,
            n => panic!("wave 15 produced {n} bosses"),
        }
    }
    assert!(saw_both.0 && saw_both.1, "the coin flip stopped being a coin flip");
}

#[test]
fn a_rolled_boss_is_announced_as_what_it_actually_is() {
    // The announcement is the only warning the player gets, and two of the
    // traits are decided rather than drawn. Naming the roll would promise a
    // hazard the boss does not leave and hide the brood it always has.
    let mut rng = Rng::new(99);
    for _ in 0..200 {
        let rolled = Recipe::roll(ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE, &mut rng);
        let named = rolled.as_boss();
        assert!(named.brood, "every one of them broods");
        assert!(!named.shed, "and none of them sheds");
        let label = named.label();
        assert!(label.contains("BROOD"), "the name hides the brood: {label}");
        assert!(!label.contains("TRAP"), "the name promises a hazard: {label}");
    }
}

/* ---------------- the bands ---------------- */

#[test]
fn the_bands_and_the_name_say_the_same_thing() {
    // The stripes are the announcement drawn instead of written, and they go on
    // saying it after the name has faded. A trait in one and not the other
    // would make them two different claims about the same enemy.
    let mut rng = Rng::new(20260822);
    let mut marks = [Rgb::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0); MAX_MARKS];
    for _ in 0..500 {
        let recipe = Recipe::roll(40, &mut rng);
        let words = recipe.label().split_whitespace().count();
        let bands = recipe.marks(&mut marks);
        assert_eq!(bands, words, "{} reads as {words} and draws as {bands}", recipe.label());
        assert!(bands >= 1 && bands <= MAX_MARKS);
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_band_order_follows_the_name() {
    // Size, then how it moves, then what it does - the order the name reads in.
    let recipe = Recipe {
        size: Size::Large,
        movement: MoveKind::Leap,
        shoot: true,
        blink: false,
        shed: false,
        brood: true,
    };
    let mut marks = [Rgb::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0); MAX_MARKS];
    let n = recipe.marks(&mut marks);
    assert_eq!(recipe.label(), "HUGE LEAPER GUN BROOD");
    assert_eq!(n, 4);
    assert_eq!(
        &marks[..4],
        &[recipe_marks::HUGE, recipe_marks::LEAPER, recipe_marks::GUN, recipe_marks::BROOD]
    );

    // The ordinary size has no word and no band, the same absence twice.
    let plain = Recipe { size: Size::Normal, ..recipe };
    assert_eq!(plain.marks(&mut marks), 3);
    assert_eq!(marks[0], recipe_marks::LEAPER);
}

#[test]
fn no_two_marks_can_be_mistaken_for_each_other_or_for_the_ground() {
    // Eyeballed sets kept putting a blue next to the lull sky. This is the
    // measurement that stopped that, kept so a future edit has to face it too.
    let marks = [
        recipe_marks::HUGE,
        recipe_marks::SMALL,
        recipe_marks::SLIGHT,
        recipe_marks::RUNNER,
        recipe_marks::HOPPER,
        recipe_marks::LEAPER,
        recipe_marks::FLIER,
        recipe_marks::GUN,
        recipe_marks::BLINK,
        recipe_marks::TRAP,
        recipe_marks::BROOD,
    ];
    // Weighted so green counts for more than blue, roughly as an eye does.
    let apart = |a: Rgb, b: Rgb| {
        let rm = (a.r + b.r) / 2.0;
        let (dr, dg, db) = (a.r - b.r, a.g - b.g, a.b - b.b);
        libm::sqrtf((2.0 + rm / 256.0) * dr * dr + 4.0 * dg * dg
            + (2.0 + (255.0 - rm) / 256.0) * db * db)
    };
    for (i, a) in marks.iter().enumerate() {
        for b in marks.iter().skip(i + 1) {
            assert!(apart(*a, *b) > 150.0, "{a:?} and {b:?} are too close");
        }
        // Against the body it is drawn on and everything behind it.
        for ground in [
            ELITE_COLOR,
            Rgb::new(240.0, 50.0, 20.0),
            Rgb::new(80.0, 170.0, 255.0),
            Rgb::new(80.0, 255.0, 130.0),
            Rgb::new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
        ] {
            assert!(apart(*a, ground) > 150.0, "{a:?} vanishes on {ground:?}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn only_the_heavies_are_banded() {
    // Young wear the same shade as the parent and are left flat. That is the
    // difference the player needs at a glance: the striped one is the one worth
    // the swings.
    let mut game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let recipe = Recipe {
        movement: MoveKind::Run,
        size: Size::Normal,
        shoot: false,
        blink: false,
        shed: false,
        brood: true,
    };
    let heavy = recipe.build(&v, 20, 0, &mut game.rng);
    let minion = recipe.build_minion(&v, 0, &mut game.rng);
    assert!(heavy.elite && heavy.recipe.is_some(), "a heavy carries what it was rolled from");
    assert!(!minion.elite && !minion.is_boss, "and a minion is neither");
    assert_eq!(heavy.color, minion.color, "but they share a shade");
}

/* ---------------- what the last level buys ---------------- */

/// One enemy standing where a swing will reach it.
fn enemy_in_front(game: &mut Game) -> usize {
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    z.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + game.players[0].body.w;
    z.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - z.body.h;
    z.ax = 0.0;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    game.zombies.push(z);
    0
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_shot_goes_through_what_it_hits() {
    // The line is what the level buys. A shot that stopped on the first enemy
    // would just be a shot.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Bullet, MAX_LEVEL);
    game.tick(&attack_input());
    let bullet = game.bullets.first().expect("a shot should have been fired");
    assert_eq!(bullet.pierces_left, BULLET_PIERCES);
    let full = bullet.damage;

    let mut bullet = game.bullets.remove(0);
    // It stops on what it hits, like any shot, and works there.
    bullet.spend_on_hit();
    assert!(!bullet.flying(), "it should stop on what it reached");
    // And when its time there is up, it sets off again for the next one.
    assert!(bullet.resume(), "a mastered shot carries on");
    assert!(bullet.flying(), "and is travelling again");
    assert!(bullet.damage < full, "worth less to the next one than to the first");

    for _ in 0..BULLET_PIERCES {
        bullet.spend_on_hit();
        if !bullet.resume() {
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!("the budget has to run out");
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_shot_still_stops_on_the_first_thing() {
    for level in 1..MAX_LEVEL {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Bullet, level);
        game.tick(&attack_input());
        let mut bullet = game.bullets.remove(0);
        assert_eq!(bullet.pierces_left, 0, "level {level} should not pierce");
        bullet.spend_on_hit();
        assert!(!bullet.flying(), "level {level} should stop");
        assert!(!bullet.resume(), "level {level} should not carry on");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_shot_that_passed_through_pays_for_it_at_the_next_target_and_not_this_one() {
    // The falloff belongs to whatever it reaches next. Charged the other way
    // round, the enemy that stopped it would be the one given the discount.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Bullet, MAX_LEVEL);
    game.tick(&attack_input());
    let first = game.bullets[0].damage;
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    z.body = game.bullets[0].body;
    z.hp = 100_000.0;
    z.hpmax = z.hp;
    z.ax = 0.0;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.zombies.push(z);
    let before = game.zombies[0].hp;

    game.tick(&idle());
    let dealt = before - game.zombies[0].hp;
    assert!((dealt - first).abs() < 1.0, "it took {dealt}, the shot was worth {first}");

    // The falloff is charged when it sets off again, not while it is still
    // working on what it stopped against - charged the other way round, the
    // enemy that stopped it would be the one given the discount.
    assert!(
        game.bullets.first().is_some_and(|b| (b.damage - first).abs() < 1.0),
        "it should still be worth full value here"
    );
    let mut bullet = game.bullets.remove(0);
    assert!(bullet.resume());
    assert!(bullet.damage < first, "and less to whatever is further along");
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_lunge_reaches_further_out_of_a_dash() {
    let reach_after = |dashing: bool| {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Lunge, MAX_LEVEL);
        if dashing {
            game.players[0].dash_ticks = DASH_TICKS;
            game.players[0].dash_dir = 1.0;
        }
        let mut frame = idle();
        frame.players[0].attack = true;
        game.tick(&frame);
        // Measured across the swing, not after it: the lunge is longest on its
        // last tick and the box goes back to resting size the tick after that.
        let mut widest = 0.0f32;
        for _ in 0..ATTACK_TICKS {
            game.tick(&idle());
            widest = widest.max(game.players[0].gun.w);
        }
        widest
    };
    let plain = reach_after(false);
    let out_of_dash = reach_after(true);
    assert!(
        out_of_dash > plain * 1.1,
        "a lunge out of a dash reached {out_of_dash}, an ordinary one {plain}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_dash_bonus_is_decided_once_and_not_read_every_tick() {
    // The grace expires partway through a swing. Read live, the reach would
    // shrink halfway out - a lunge that gets shorter the longer it is held.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Lunge, MAX_LEVEL);
    game.players[0].dash_ticks = DASH_TICKS;
    game.players[0].dash_dir = 1.0;
    let mut frame = idle();
    frame.players[0].attack = true;
    game.tick(&frame);
    assert!(game.players[0].swing_from_dash);

    let mut widest = 0.0f32;
    for _ in 0..ATTACK_TICKS {
        game.tick(&idle());
        widest = widest.max(game.players[0].gun.w);
    }
    assert!(game.players[0].swing_from_dash, "it should hold for the whole swing");
    assert!(widest > 0.0);
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_lunge_is_untouched_by_the_dash() {
    for level in 1..MAX_LEVEL {
        let with = |dashing: bool| {
            AttackKind::Lunge.length_scale(level, 1.0, dashing)
        };
        assert_eq!(with(true), with(false), "level {level} should not care");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_sweep_throws_what_it_touches_clear() {
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Thin.knockback_scale(1), 1.0);
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Thin.knockback_scale(2), 1.0);
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Thin.knockback_scale(MAX_LEVEL), 2.0);

    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Thin, MAX_LEVEL);
    enemy_in_front(&mut game);
    let target = game.zombies[0].body;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    game.tick(&idle());
    let fast = game.zombies[0].ax.abs();

    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Thin, 1);
    enemy_in_front(&mut game);
    let target = game.zombies[0].body;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    game.tick(&idle());
    let slow = game.zombies[0].ax.abs();
    assert!(fast > slow * 1.5, "mastered threw at {fast}, level one at {slow}");
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_single_hit_finishes_what_is_nearly_down() {
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::SingleHit, MAX_LEVEL);
    enemy_in_front(&mut game);
    // Too much health for the blow itself, little enough to be finished.
    game.zombies[0].hpmax = 100_000.0;
    game.zombies[0].hp = 100_000.0 * (EXECUTE_HP_FRACTION - 0.05);
    let target = game.zombies[0].body;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    // Damage lands on this tick; the kill is confirmed on the next pass over
    // the list, which is where the payout and the refund both live. Looked for
    // by its health rather than by the list being empty - the wave goes on
    // feeding the field while this runs, and a fresh enemy is not the one that
    // was hit.
    game.tick(&idle());
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack_ticks, 0, "the swing should be ready again");
    let left: alloc::vec::Vec<f32> = game
        .zombies
        .iter()
        .filter(|z| z.hpmax == 100_000.0)
        .map(|z| z.hp)
        .collect();
    assert!(left.is_empty(), "it survived on {left:?}");
}

#[test]
fn there_is_no_execution_bonus_at_full_health() {
    // The weapon must not become the best opener - that is the one thing it is
    // not for.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::SingleHit, MAX_LEVEL);
    enemy_in_front(&mut game);
    game.zombies[0].hpmax = 100_000.0;
    game.zombies[0].hp = 100_000.0;
    let target = game.zombies[0].body;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    game.tick(&idle());
    let dealt = 100_000.0 - game.zombies[0].hp;
    let plain = AttackKind::SingleHit.damage(MAX_LEVEL);
    assert!((dealt - plain).abs() < 1.0, "it dealt {dealt}, an ordinary blow is {plain}");
}

#[test]
fn a_heavy_target_is_hurt_more_rather_than_finished() {
    // Soft counterplay: better at the job it is for, still worth carrying
    // against the job it is not.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::SingleHit, MAX_LEVEL);
    enemy_in_front(&mut game);
    game.zombies[0].elite = true;
    game.zombies[0].hpmax = 100_000.0;
    game.zombies[0].hp = 100_000.0 * (EXECUTE_HP_FRACTION - 0.05);
    let before = game.zombies[0].hp;
    let target = game.zombies[0].body;
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(!game.zombies.is_empty(), "an elite is not finished outright");
    let dealt = before - game.zombies[0].hp;
    let plain = AttackKind::SingleHit.damage(MAX_LEVEL);
    assert!(dealt > plain, "it should still hurt more: {dealt} against {plain}");
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_hammer_leaves_a_wave_and_a_plain_one_does_not() {
    let blasts = |level: u8| {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Hammer, level);
        enemy_in_front(&mut game);
        game.zombies[0].hpmax = 100_000.0;
        game.zombies[0].hp = 100_000.0;
        let target = game.zombies[0].body;
        swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
        game.tick(&idle());
        game.explosions.iter().filter(|e| e.damage > 0.0).count()
    };
    assert_eq!(blasts(1), 0, "level one is a blow and nothing else");
    assert_eq!(blasts(MAX_LEVEL), 1, "mastered, it leaves one wave");
}

#[test]
fn the_hammer_wave_is_not_a_second_swing() {
    // It reaches what stood beside what was hit. A wave worth its own swing
    // would make the hammer the only weapon worth carrying.
    assert!(
        HAMMER_WAVE_DAMAGE < AttackKind::Hammer.damage(MAX_LEVEL) / 4.0,
        "the wave is too close to the blow itself"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_heavy_ends_a_flight_however_it_was_flying() {
    let v = Viewport::new(1280.0, 800.0);
    let dropped = |movement: Movement| {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Tall, MAX_LEVEL);
        enemy_in_front(&mut game);
        game.zombies[0].movement = movement;
        game.zombies[0].body.y -= v.hper(20.0);
        let target = game.zombies[0].body;
        swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
        game.tick(&idle());
        game.zombies[0].movement
    };
    assert!(matches!(
        dropped(Movement::Hop { cooldown: 0 }),
        Movement::Hop { cooldown } if cooldown == JUMPER_JUMP_EVERY
    ));
    assert!(matches!(
        dropped(Movement::Leap(Leap { crouch: 0, airborne: true })),
        Movement::Leap(Leap { airborne: false, .. })
    ));
    // A flier's height is not a position; it is recomputed from a sine every
    // tick, so what moves is where it sits in that wave.
    let before = Movement::Fly { offset: 1000 };
    let Movement::Fly { offset } = dropped(before) else {
        panic!("a flier should still be flying");
    };
    assert_ne!(offset, 1000, "it should have been carried along its arc");
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_heavy_leaves_a_flight_alone() {
    for level in 1..MAX_LEVEL {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Tall, level);
        let v = game.viewport;
        enemy_in_front(&mut game);
        game.zombies[0].movement = Movement::Leap(Leap { crouch: 0, airborne: true });
        // Off the ground, or it lands of its own accord and proves nothing.
        game.zombies[0].body.y -= v.hper(20.0);
        let target = game.zombies[0].body;
        swing_at(&mut game, 0, target);
        game.tick(&idle());
        assert!(
            matches!(game.zombies[0].movement, Movement::Leap(Leap { airborne: true, .. })),
            "level {level} should not ground anything"
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_trap_is_a_gate_from_either_side() {
    // A barrier has to work from both sides or it is just a slow patch of
    // floor. Away from the trap, not away from the player.
    let side = |from_left: bool| {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Frozen, MAX_LEVEL);
        game.tick(&attack_input());
        let trap = game.traps[0].body;
        assert!(game.traps[0].shoves);
        let v = game.viewport;
        let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
        z.body = trap;
        z.body.x = if from_left { trap.x - 1.0 } else { trap.x + trap.w - z.body.w + 1.0 };
        z.ax = 0.0;
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.zombies.push(z);
        game.tick(&idle());
        game.zombies[0].ax
    };
    assert!(side(true) < 0.0, "something entering from the left goes back left");
    assert!(side(false) > 0.0, "and from the right, back right");
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_trap_only_nudges() {
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Frozen, 1);
    game.tick(&attack_input());
    assert!(!game.traps[0].shoves, "the gate is what the last level buys");
}

#[test]
fn a_shot_stops_at_two_in_the_air() {
    // Two, not three. For a bullet the step from the second level to the third
    // is a change in behaviour, and another instance on top would make it twice
    // the size of the step before it.
    //
    // A trap does not work that way: what its levels buy *is* instances, so it
    // keeps growing. See `each_level_lays_more_floor_than_the_last`.
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Bullet.max_instances(1), 1);
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Bullet.max_instances(2), 2);
    assert_eq!(AttackKind::Bullet.max_instances(MAX_LEVEL), 2, "a shot still grew");
}

#[test]
fn a_blast_takes_the_body_it_kills() {
    // It used to leave it standing. The death path lived inside the branch that
    // runs while something is *hitting* the enemy, so anything killed by a
    // blast alone stayed on the field at negative health - still chasing, still
    // hurting, and counting against the wave clearing. A combo finisher has
    // been able to strand a body this way for as long as there has been one.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Basic, 1);
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    z.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + v.wper(30.0);
    z.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - z.body.h;
    z.hp = 1.0;
    z.hpmax = 255.0;
    z.ax = 0.0;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    game.zombies.push(z);
    let (bx, by) = (game.zombies[0].body.x, game.zombies[0].body.y);
    let score = game.players[0].score;
    game.explosions
        .push(Explosion::lethal(bx, by, &v, 500.0, 4242, 0));

    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(
        !game.zombies.iter().any(|z| z.hpmax == 255.0 && z.hp < 0.0),
        "a body was left standing"
    );
    assert!(game.players[0].score > score, "and the blast's owner should be paid");
}

#[test]
fn a_blast_with_no_owner_still_clears_the_body() {
    // A corpse left standing is a worse outcome than a score nobody was
    // credited with.
    let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Basic, 1);
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
    z.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + v.wper(30.0);
    z.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - z.body.h;
    z.hp = 1.0;
    z.hpmax = 255.0;
    z.ax = 0.0;
    game.zombies.clear();
    game.flyers.clear();
    game.zombies.push(z);
    let (bx, by) = (game.zombies[0].body.x, game.zombies[0].body.y);
    let mut blast = Explosion::lethal(bx, by, &v, 500.0, 4242, 0);
    blast.owner = None;
    game.explosions.push(blast);

    game.tick(&idle());
    assert!(!game.zombies.iter().any(|z| z.hpmax == 255.0 && z.hp < 0.0));
}

#[test]
fn no_last_level_is_a_downgrade() {
    // Damage put into one pinned target over a fixed window. Not a time to
    // kill: the throw carries an enemy out of the box and back, so a kill time
    // measures that cycle more than it measures the weapon.
    //
    // Two of these were downgrades when they were first written, and both were
    // found here rather than by reading: a mastered shot that flew through gave
    // up the stop every other shot makes, and a mastered hammer traded strike
    // numbers with its own wave and sawed through a target ten times faster
    // than anything else.
    const WINDOW: i32 = 600;
    let throughput = |kind: AttackKind, level: u8| -> f32 {
        let mut game = armed_with(kind, level);
        let v = game.viewport;
        let mut z = Zombie::from_edge(&v, &mut game.rng, TEST_ENEMY_COLOR);
        z.hp = 1_000_000.0;
        z.hpmax = 1_000_000.0;
        z.speed_multiplier = 0.0;
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.zombies.push(z);
        for _ in 0..WINDOW {
            game.players[0].hp = game.players[0].hpmax;
            game.players[0].dead = false;
            if let Some(zi) = game.zombies.iter().position(|z| z.hpmax == 1_000_000.0) {
                game.zombies[zi].body.x = game.players[0].body.x + game.players[0].body.w;
                game.zombies[zi].body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - game.zombies[zi].body.h;
                game.zombies[zi].ax = 0.0;
                game.zombies[zi].ay = 0.0;
            }
            if !game.players[0].attacking() {
                game.tick(&attack_input());
            } else {
                game.tick(&idle());
            }
        }
        game
            .zombies
            .iter()
            .find(|z| z.hpmax == 1_000_000.0)
            .map(|z| 1_000_000.0 - z.hp)
            .unwrap_or(0.0)
    };

    for kind in AttackKind::ALL {
        let (one, two, three) = (
            throughput(kind, 1),
            throughput(kind, 2),
            throughput(kind, MAX_LEVEL),
        );
        assert!(
            three >= two && two >= one,
            "{}: {one} -> {two} -> {three} goes backwards",
            kind.label()
        );
        // And no step that dwarfs the weapon it belongs to. A last level worth
        // several of its own weapon is not a specialisation, it is a different
        // weapon that happens to share a name.
        //
        // The trap is the one exception, and it is a deliberate one. Its first
        // two levels leave damage alone and buy floor instead, so the whole of
        // its number lands on the last step: 16 per tick to 64, exactly four
        // times, by construction rather than by drift. Measured on one enemy in
        // one box this probe sees only that - it cannot see the four boxes
        // becoming ten, or the life becoming endless, so the real distance
        // between the ends of this ladder is wider still. Those two axes are
        // checked in `each_level_lays_more_floor_than_the_last`.
        let limit = if kind == AttackKind::Frozen { 4.0 } else { 4.0 - f32::EPSILON };
        assert!(
            three <= one * limit,
            "{}: the last level is {three} against {one} at the first",
            kind.label()
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_blind_wave_shows_its_enemies_twice_a_second() {
    // Two frames in sixty. Long enough to read where a crowd is, short enough
    // that the reading is out of date by the time it is acted on.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    game.waves.rule = WaveRule::Hidden;
    let mut seen = 0;
    for t in 0..BLIND_GLIMPSE_EVERY * 3 {
        game.timer = t;
        if game.enemies_visible() {
            seen += 1;
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(seen, BLIND_GLIMPSE_TICKS * 3, "three seconds, two frames each");

    // And the frames are consecutive rather than scattered through the second.
    game.timer = 0;
    assert!(game.enemies_visible());
    game.timer = 1;
    assert!(game.enemies_visible());
    game.timer = 2;
    assert!(!game.enemies_visible());
}

#[test]
fn every_other_wave_shows_its_enemies_all_the_time() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    for rule in [
        WaveRule::Normal,
        WaveRule::StaticCamera,
        WaveRule::NoJumps,
        WaveRule::NoWall,
    ] {
        game.waves.rule = rule;
        for t in 0..BLIND_GLIMPSE_EVERY {
            game.timer = t;
            assert!(game.enemies_visible(), "{rule:?} hid something at tick {t}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_shot_carries_on_the_way_it_was_going() {
    // It used to come back. `stop` zeroes `ax`, and the direction was read back
    // off it - and zero reads as "rightwards", so every shot fired leftwards
    // turned round at the first thing it hit. Fired rightwards it looked
    // perfectly correct, which is why it took a player to notice.
    for facing_right in [true, false] {
        let mut game = armed_with(AttackKind::Bullet, MAX_LEVEL);
        game.players[0].facing_right = facing_right;
        game.tick(&attack_input());
        let mut bullet = game.bullets.remove(0);
        let outward = bullet.ax;
        assert!(outward != 0.0);

        bullet.spend_on_hit();
        assert_eq!(bullet.ax, 0.0, "a shot stops on what it reaches");
        assert!(bullet.resume(), "and a mastered one sets off again");
        assert_eq!(
            bullet.ax.signum(),
            outward.signum(),
            "facing_right={facing_right}: it turned round"
        );
    }
}

/* ---------------- the campaign ---------------- */

/// A campaign for `weapon`, standing in its first lull.
fn campaign_run(weapon: AttackKind) -> Game {
    written_run(ModeKind::Campaign, weapon)
}

/// A written run of either mode for `weapon`, standing in its first lull.
fn written_run(kind: ModeKind, weapon: AttackKind) -> Game {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_written(kind, weapon, 1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game
}

/// The same, without paying. A tutorial's offers are paced by its waves rather
/// than by its score, so this is the only way to see them.
fn next_offer_unpaid(game: &mut Game) -> Option<alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem>> {
    into_the_lull(game);
    let out = game
        .offer
        .as_ref()
        .map(|o| o.choices.iter().take(o.count).map(|c| c.item).collect());
    // Take the first one, so the run carries what the script gave it.
    if let Some(offer) = game.offer.as_ref() {
        if offer.count > 0 {
            let body = offer.choices[0].body;
            wait_for_armed(game);
            swing_at(game, 0, body);
            game.tick(&idle());
        }
    }
    game.offer = None;
    game.waves.skip_countdown();
    out
}

/// Ends the wave in progress at once, by spending its budget for it.
///
/// A campaign runs a dozen waves and more, and by then a wave's budget takes
/// longer to spawn than [`into_the_lull`] will wait. Nothing here is testing
/// how a wave paces itself, so the budget is simply declared spent.
fn skip_the_wave(game: &mut Game) {
    game.spawn_count = wave_budget(game.wave);
    for _ in 0..600 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.waves.between_waves() || game.result.is_some() {
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!("the wave never cleared");
}

/// Clears a wave and reports what stood in the lull, taking the weapon upgrade
/// if one was offered and the first box otherwise.
///
/// Taking the weapon on sight is the short, thin campaign - which is exactly
/// the run these tests want, because it is the one that reaches the end.
fn next_campaign_offer(game: &mut Game) -> Option<alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem>> {
    // The offer is triggered by score, so give it one.
    game.players[0].score += crate::offer::OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    skip_the_wave(game);
    let Some(offer) = game.offer.as_ref() else {
        game.waves.skip_countdown();
        return None;
    };
    let items: alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem> =
        offer.choices.iter().take(offer.count).map(|c| c.item).collect();
    let pick = items
        .iter()
        .position(|i| matches!(i, OfferItem::Attack { .. }))
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let body = offer.choices[pick].body;
    wait_for_armed(game);
    swing_at(game, 0, body);
    game.tick(&idle());
    game.offer = None;
    game.waves.skip_countdown();
    Some(items)
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_offers_boons_and_walls_beside_its_weapon() {
    // The whole point of the mode is that it is a real run with one weapon in
    // it, not a delivery route for three upgrades.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Hammer);
    let mut saw_boon = false;
    let mut saw_wall = false;
    for _ in 0..12 {
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        for item in offered {
            match item {
                OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPull | Boon::WallPush) => saw_wall = true,
                OfferItem::Boon(_) => saw_boon = true,
                OfferItem::Attack { .. } => {}
                OfferItem::Rule(r) => panic!("a campaign offered terms: {r:?}"),
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(saw_boon, "a campaign never offered a boon");
    assert!(saw_wall, "a campaign never offered a wall");
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_never_offers_another_weapon() {
    // The whole shape of the mode: one weapon, learned, rather than a handful
    // collected. It is the roster the campaign takes away, not the choosing.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Tall);
    for _ in 0..12 {
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        for item in offered {
            if let OfferItem::Attack { kind, .. } = item {
                assert_eq!(kind, AttackKind::Tall, "a second weapon was offered");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_offers_the_weapon_once_per_level_and_then_stops() {
    // Level one as a pick-up, then two and three as steps, and after that the
    // pool is boons alone.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Hammer);
    let mut levels = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..14 {
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        for item in offered {
            if let OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } = item {
                assert_eq!(kind, AttackKind::Hammer);
                levels.push(level);
            }
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(levels, alloc::vec![1, 2, 3], "levels offered, in order");
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack, AttackKind::Hammer);
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack_level, MAX_LEVEL);
}

#[test]
fn a_plain_campaign_offers_no_weapon_at_all() {
    // And so keeps the combo finisher, which every weapon in the roster trades
    // away on being picked up. Its pool is boons and nothing else.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Basic);
    for _ in 0..8 {
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        for item in offered {
            assert!(
                matches!(item, OfferItem::Boon(_)),
                "the plain campaign offered {item:?}"
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_wall_already_carried_is_never_offered_back() {
    // The two walls are one setting, and both may stand together while neither
    // is held - that is the genuine choice between them. What must not happen
    // is being offered the one already carried, which would be a box that does
    // nothing.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Basic);
    game.players[0].boons.take(Boon::WallPull);
    for _ in 0..10 {
        // Read before the offer: the box taken last round may itself have been
        // the other wall, and then it is that one that must not come back.
        let held = game.players[0].boons.wall;
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        let back = match held {
            WallMod::Pull => Some(OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPull)),
            WallMod::Push => Some(OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPush)),
            WallMod::Plain => None,
        };
        if let Some(back) = back {
            assert!(
                !offered.contains(&back),
                "the wall already in hand was offered again: {offered:?}"
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn both_walls_may_stand_together_while_neither_is_held() {
    // Handing them out in turn would be a forced swap rather than a decision.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Basic);
    let mut saw_both = false;
    for _ in 0..20 {
        let held = game.players[0].boons.wall;
        let Some(offered) = next_campaign_offer(&mut game) else { continue };
        if held == WallMod::Plain
            && offered.contains(&OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPull))
            && offered.contains(&OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPush))
        {
            saw_both = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    assert!(saw_both, "the two walls never stood together");
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_pool_narrows_but_never_empties() {
    // Rolled straight rather than played to: a run this far along has already
    // met its finish, so the only way to look at the pool it would leave behind
    // is to ask for it.
    //
    // It never empties, because whichever wall is not being carried is always
    // worth swapping to. A lull with an empty box in it would be worse than a
    // quiet one, and this is why there is never one.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    let held = Boons {
        double_jump: true,
        dash_free: true,
        shield: true,
        wall: WallMod::Pull,
    };
    let mut rng = Rng::new(7);
    let offer = Offer::roll_campaign(
        &v,
        0.0,
        ground,
        AttackKind::Hammer,
        AttackKind::Hammer,
        MAX_LEVEL,
        held,
        0,
        &mut rng,
    );
    assert_eq!(offer.count, 1, "one box, since one thing is left");
    assert_eq!(offer.choices[0].item, OfferItem::Boon(Boon::WallPush));
}

#[test]
fn a_fresh_campaign_pool_is_the_weapon_and_every_boon() {
    // Six things: the weapon to pick up, three boons and two walls. Three of
    // them stand, so a campaign lull looks exactly like an ordinary one.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut rng = Rng::new(11);
    let offer = Offer::roll_campaign(
        &v,
        0.0,
        v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT),
        AttackKind::Hammer,
        AttackKind::Basic,
        1,
        Boons::default(),
        0,
        &mut rng,
    );
    assert_eq!(offer.count, crate::offer::OFFER_CHOICES, "three boxes, as anywhere else");
}

/// Runs waves until the campaign ends, and reports the wave it ended on.
fn play_out_campaign(game: &mut Game) -> i64 {
    for _ in 0..40 {
        next_campaign_offer(game);
        if let Some(result) = game.result {
            return result.wave;
        }
    }
    panic!("the campaign never ended");
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_ends_one_wave_after_the_weapon_is_mastered() {
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Lunge);
    let mut mastered_on = None;
    for _ in 0..40 {
        next_campaign_offer(&mut game);
        if mastered_on.is_none() && game.players[0].attack_level >= MAX_LEVEL {
            // `wave` is already the coming one: the lull sits between them.
            mastered_on = Some(game.wave);
        }
        if let Some(result) = game.result {
            let mastered = mastered_on.expect("it ended without ever mastering");
            assert!(result.won(), "reaching the end is the win");
            assert_eq!(
                result.wave, mastered,
                "the run should end on the first wave fought at full level"
            );
            assert!(game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Lunge).cleared);
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!("the campaign never ended");
}

#[test]
fn mastering_the_weapon_is_not_by_itself_the_win() {
    // One more wave has to be survived, and that wave is a real one.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Lunge);
    for _ in 0..40 {
        next_campaign_offer(&mut game);
        if game.players[0].attack_level >= MAX_LEVEL {
            assert!(
                game.result.is_none(),
                "the run ended in the very lull that granted the level"
            );
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!("the weapon was never mastered");
}

#[test]
fn a_plain_campaign_ends_on_carrying_everything_instead() {
    // `Basic` has no level to reach, so its campaign ends one wave after the
    // player is carrying every boon a run can carry at once.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Basic);
    let wave = play_out_campaign(&mut game);
    assert!(game.players[0].boons.full(), "it ended before the boons were in");
    assert!(wave > 1);
    assert!(game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Basic).cleared);
}

#[test]
fn dying_short_of_mastery_is_not_a_win() {
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Lunge);
    game.state = State::Playing;
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.hp = 1.0;
    }
    let source = Body::new(0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 10.0);
    game.damage_player(0, 1000.0, &source, Reach::Normal);
    let result = game.result.expect("the run should have ended");
    assert!(!result.won(), "it ended, it was not finished");
    assert!(!game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Lunge).cleared);
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_score_stays_out_of_the_endless_table() {
    // Its pool is narrowed, so its scores are not comparable with a run that
    // rolled from the whole roster.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Hammer);
    game.settings.record_solo = 0;
    game.players[0].score = 999_999;
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.hp = 1.0;
    }
    let source = Body::new(0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 10.0);
    game.damage_player(0, 1000.0, &source, Reach::Normal);
    assert_eq!(game.settings.record_solo, 0, "the endless record was touched");
    assert_eq!(
        game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Hammer).best_score,
        999_999
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_record_keeps_the_best_and_not_the_last() {
    // The point of keeping it is to answer "how far did I get", and a worse
    // attempt does not un-get the earlier one.
    let mut settings = Settings::default();
    settings.record_mode(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin, 17, 5000, false);
    settings.record_mode(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin, 4, 100, false);
    let slot = settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin);
    assert_eq!(slot.best_wave, 17);
    assert_eq!(slot.best_score, 5000);
    assert!(!slot.cleared);
    settings.record_mode(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin, 21, 200, true);
    assert!(settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin).cleared);
    settings.record_mode(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin, 2, 10, false);
    assert!(
        settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Thin).cleared,
        "cleared only ever turns on"
    );
}

#[test]
fn an_ordinary_run_is_untouched_by_any_of_this() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    assert_eq!(game.mode, RunMode::Endless);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.players[0].score = crate::offer::OFFER_SCORE_STEP;
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    let offer = game.offer.as_ref().expect("an ordinary lull still offers");
    assert_eq!(offer.count, 3, "and it still offers three");
    let kinds: alloc::vec::Vec<AttackKind> = offer
        .choices
        .iter()
        .take(offer.count)
        .filter_map(|c| match c.item {
            OfferItem::Attack { kind, .. } => Some(kind),
            OfferItem::Boon(_) | OfferItem::Rule(_) => None,
        })
        .collect();
    assert!(!kinds.is_empty(), "the whole roster is still in the pool");
}

/* ---------------- the tutorial ---------------- */

#[test]
fn a_tutorial_is_a_wave_per_level_of_its_weapon() {
    // Wave one at level one, wave two at two, wave three at three. The weapon
    // is in hand before the first wave rather than after it: a tutorial that
    // spent its opening wave on the plain swing would be teaching the thing it
    // is not about.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Hammer);
    assert_eq!(game.wave, 1);
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack, AttackKind::Hammer);
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack_level, 1, "wave one, level one");

    for level in 2..=MAX_LEVEL {
        assert_eq!(
            next_offer_unpaid(&mut game),
            Some(alloc::vec![OfferItem::Attack {
                kind: AttackKind::Hammer,
                level
            }])
        );
        assert_eq!(game.wave, level as i64, "wave {level}");
        assert_eq!(game.players[0].attack_level, level);
    }
    assert_eq!(game.wave, crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE);
}

#[test]
fn a_tutorial_offers_nothing_but_its_weapon() {
    // No boons, no walls, no choices. Three waves is a lesson, and a lesson
    // with a decision in it is teaching two things at once.
    for weapon in AttackKind::ALL {
        for step in crate::campaign::tutorial_script(weapon) {
            assert_eq!(step.len(), 1, "{weapon:?}: a step with a choice in it");
            assert!(
                matches!(step[0], OfferItem::Attack { kind, .. } if kind == weapon),
                "{weapon:?} offered {:?}",
                step[0]
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_plain_tutorial_teaches_two_boons_instead() {
    // `Basic` has no weapon to be given, so it spends both steps on the two
    // things the plain swing has to work with.
    let script = crate::campaign::tutorial_script(AttackKind::Basic);
    assert_eq!(script.len(), 2);
    for step in &script {
        for item in step {
            assert!(
                matches!(item, OfferItem::Boon(_)),
                "the plain tutorial offered {item:?}"
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_tutorial_offer_is_not_paid_for() {
    // Three waves' worth of score does not reach the first threshold, so a
    // tutorial that charged for its offers would never hand over the weapon it
    // exists to teach.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Tall);
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.score = 0;
    }
    assert!(
        game.total_score() < OFFER_SCORE_STEP,
        "the premise: nothing has been earned yet"
    );
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_some(), "the lull should be offering anyway");
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_offer_is_still_paid_for() {
    // The other half of the rule above: only the tutorial is free.
    let mut game = campaign_run(AttackKind::Tall);
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.score = 0;
    }
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "a campaign offer has to be earned");
}

#[test]
fn a_tutorial_runs_plain_waves_whatever_the_roll_says() {
    // Three waves is not enough room for a variant to be met and understood,
    // and being handed BLIND on the wave that teaches the weapon would teach
    // nothing at all. Rolled two hundred times rather than walked through
    // three: walking three waves would pass on a seed that happened to roll
    // plain ones, which is most seeds.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge);
    assert_eq!(game.waves.rule, WaveRule::Normal, "the wave in progress");
    assert_eq!(game.waves.kind, WaveKind::Mixed, "the wave in progress");
    for wave in 1..=200 {
        game.waves.begin_wave(wave, &mut game.rng);
        assert_eq!(game.waves.rule, WaveRule::Normal, "wave {wave}");
        assert_eq!(game.waves.kind, WaveKind::Mixed, "wave {wave}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_after_a_tutorial_is_not_still_pinned() {
    // The pins survive `WaveManager::reset` on purpose, so the run that starts
    // next has to be the one that clears them - or a tutorial would quietly
    // flatten every campaign played after it in the same session.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge);
    assert!(game.waves.forced_rule.is_some(), "the premise");
    game.start_written(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Lunge, 1);
    assert_eq!(game.waves.forced_rule, None);
    assert_eq!(game.waves.forced_kind, None);
    game.start_run(1);
    assert_eq!(game.waves.forced_rule, None);
}

#[test]
fn clearing_the_third_wave_wins_the_tutorial() {
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge);
    game.wave = crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE;
    game.spawn_count = wave_budget(game.wave);
    for _ in 0..600 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.result.is_some() {
            break;
        }
    }
    let result = game.result.expect("the run should have ended");
    assert!(result.won(), "clearing the last wave is the win");
    assert_eq!(result.headline(), "TUTORIAL CLEAR");
    assert!(game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge).cleared);
}

#[test]
fn a_tutorial_does_not_clear_the_campaign_beside_it() {
    // Eighteen separate things to finish, and the screen has to be able to say
    // which. One column filling in must not fill in the other.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge);
    game.wave = crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE;
    game.spawn_count = wave_budget(game.wave);
    for _ in 0..600 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.result.is_some() {
            break;
        }
    }
    assert!(game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge).cleared);
    assert!(
        !game.settings.mode_record(ModeKind::Campaign, AttackKind::Lunge).cleared,
        "the campaign was cleared by its tutorial"
    );
}

#[test]
fn every_tutorial_has_a_lull_for_every_step_it_owes() {
    // Three waves have two lulls, so a script of more than two steps would end
    // with something it never got to hand over.
    for weapon in AttackKind::EVERY {
        let steps = crate::campaign::tutorial_steps(weapon) as i64;
        assert_eq!(
            steps,
            crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE - 1,
            "{weapon:?}: {steps} steps for {} waves",
            crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_written_screen_starts_what_it_names() {
    use crate::menu::MenuAction;
    // The mode row and the weapon row both feed START. Before the screen had
    // two rows to move between, `selected_action` read the title menu's index
    // instead of this screen's - which meant moving the highlight moved
    // nothing at all.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.state = State::Campaigns;
    game.campaign_menu.index = 0;
    let rows = game.menu_rows([false; MAX_PLAYERS]);
    assert!(rows[0].label.starts_with("MODE:"));
    assert!(rows[1].label.starts_with("WEAPON:"));

    // Flip the mode row, then walk down to START and press it.
    let mut right = idle();
    right.menu.right = true;
    game.tick(&right);
    assert_eq!(game.campaign_mode_choice(), ModeKind::Campaign);

    let start = rows
        .iter()
        .position(|r| r.action == MenuAction::StartCampaign)
        .expect("the screen should offer START");
    game.campaign_menu.index = start;
    let weapon = game.campaign_choice();
    let mut confirm = idle();
    confirm.menu.confirm = true;
    game.tick(&confirm);
    assert_eq!(game.mode, RunMode::Written(ModeKind::Campaign, weapon));
    assert_eq!(game.state, State::Playing);
}

#[test]
fn the_status_line_follows_the_mode_row() {
    // Nine weapons in two modes are eighteen separate records, and the screen
    // must show the one it is pointing at.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.state = State::Campaigns;
    let weapon = game.campaign_choice();
    game.settings.record_mode(ModeKind::Tutorial, weapon, 3, 400, true);
    assert_eq!(game.campaign_mode_choice(), ModeKind::Tutorial);
    assert_eq!(game.campaign_status(), "CLEARED - BEST 400");
    game.campaign_menu.index = 0;
    let mut right = idle();
    right.menu.right = true;
    game.tick(&right);
    assert_eq!(game.campaign_mode_choice(), ModeKind::Campaign);
    assert_eq!(game.campaign_status(), "NOT ATTEMPTED");
}

#[test]
fn a_finished_written_run_returns_to_the_screen_it_came_from() {
    // Where the record it has just written is now on the line under the menu.
    let mut game = written_run(ModeKind::Tutorial, AttackKind::Lunge);
    game.wave = crate::campaign::TUTORIAL_LAST_WAVE;
    game.spawn_count = wave_budget(game.wave);
    for _ in 0..600 {
        game.zombies.clear();
        game.flyers.clear();
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.awaiting_dismiss() {
            break;
        }
    }
    assert!(game.awaiting_dismiss(), "the result should be waiting");
    let mut confirm = idle();
    confirm.menu.confirm = true;
    game.tick(&confirm);
    assert_eq!(game.state, State::Campaigns);
    assert_eq!(game.mode, RunMode::Endless, "the next run is not still written");
}

#[test]
fn a_finished_endless_run_still_returns_to_the_title() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.hp = 1.0;
    }
    let source = Body::new(0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 10.0);
    game.damage_player(0, 1000.0, &source, Reach::Normal);
    for _ in 0..600 {
        game.tick(&idle());
        if game.awaiting_dismiss() {
            break;
        }
    }
    let mut confirm = idle();
    confirm.menu.confirm = true;
    game.tick(&confirm);
    assert_eq!(game.state, State::Title);
}

/* ---------------- the airborne wave ---------------- */

/// Holds a direction for `ticks` and reports how far the player travelled.
fn walked(game: &mut Game, right: bool, ticks: usize) -> f32 {
    let mut walk = idle();
    walk.players[0].right = right;
    walk.players[0].left = !right;
    let from = game.players[0].body.x;
    for _ in 0..ticks {
        clear_arena(game);
        game.tick(&walk);
    }
    game.players[0].body.x - from
}

#[test]
fn a_wave_with_no_footing_takes_the_ground_out_from_under_a_step() {
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::NoFooting);
    assert!(game.players[0].grounded, "the premise: standing on the floor");
    assert_eq!(walked(&mut game, true, 30), 0.0, "it walked anyway");
}

#[test]
