fn a_wave_with_no_footing_still_lets_the_player_turn_on_the_spot() {
    // Turning has to keep working or the wave would take away aiming as well,
    // which is not what it is about: a swing and a dash both go whichever way
    // the player is facing.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::NoFooting);
    game.players[0].facing_right = true;

    let mut left = idle();
    left.players[0].left = true;
    clear_arena(&mut game);
    game.tick(&left);
    assert!(!game.players[0].facing_right, "it would not turn to face left");
    assert_eq!(game.players[0].ax, 0.0, "and it must not have moved doing it");

    let mut right = idle();
    right.players[0].right = true;
    clear_arena(&mut game);
    game.tick(&right);
    assert!(game.players[0].facing_right, "nor back to the right");
}

#[test]
fn a_wave_with_no_footing_gives_the_step_back_once_the_player_leaves_the_floor() {
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::NoFooting);
    let mut jump = idle();
    jump.players[0].jump = true;
    jump.players[0].right = true;
    clear_arena(&mut game);
    game.tick(&jump);
    assert!(!game.players[0].grounded, "the premise: off the floor");

    let moved = walked(&mut game, true, 6);
    assert!(moved > 0.0, "airborne, a held direction should carry: {moved}");
}

#[test]
fn a_wave_with_no_footing_leaves_the_dash_alone() {
    // Deliberate: a rule with no answer to standing in a bad place is a trap
    // rather than a constraint. The dash costs energy and holds a cooldown, so
    // it is a way off the floor sideways that still has to be paid for - and
    // it is pointed by the facing, which is why turning stays.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::NoFooting);
    let from = game.players[0].body.x;
    let mut dash = idle();
    dash.players[0].dash = true;
    clear_arena(&mut game);
    game.tick(&dash);
    for _ in 0..DASH_TICKS {
        clear_arena(&mut game);
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    assert!(
        (game.players[0].body.x - from).abs() > 0.0,
        "the dash should still carry on an airborne wave"
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_wave_with_no_footing_still_allows_the_jump_it_depends_on() {
    // Otherwise the wave is not a restriction, it is a locked room.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::NoFooting);
    let mut jump = idle();
    jump.players[0].jump = true;
    clear_arena(&mut game);
    game.tick(&jump);
    assert!(!game.players[0].grounded, "the one way out is shut");
}

#[test]
fn every_other_wave_still_walks() {
    // The gate reads the rule, not the ground, so nothing else may have picked
    // up a limp.
    for rule in [
        WaveRule::Normal,
        WaveRule::StaticCamera,
        WaveRule::NoJumps,
        WaveRule::NoWall,
        WaveRule::Hidden,
    ] {
        let mut game = game_with_rule(rule);
        let moved = walked(&mut game, true, 20);
        assert!(moved > 0.0, "{rule:?} stopped the player walking: {moved}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_modifier_but_the_plain_one_announces_itself() {
    // The countdown is the only place a wave says what it is about to do, so a
    // rule that got added without a label would arrive as an unexplained change
    // in the controls.
    for rule in [
        WaveRule::StaticCamera,
        WaveRule::NoJumps,
        WaveRule::NoWall,
        WaveRule::Hidden,
        WaveRule::NoFooting,
    ] {
        let label = rule.label().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{rule:?} says nothing"));
        assert!(!label.is_empty());
        // Not the wave *kind*'s words. The two are announced one under the
        // other, and a rule that borrowed a kind's phrasing would read as a
        // contradiction on screen.
        for kind in [
            WaveKind::GroundOnly,
            WaveKind::FlyersOnly,
            WaveKind::BasicOnly,
            WaveKind::JumpersOnly,
        ] {
            assert_ne!(Some(label), kind.label(), "{rule:?} and {kind:?} say the same thing");
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(WaveRule::Normal.label(), None, "a plain wave says nothing");
}

/* ---------------- swapping weapons ---------------- */

/// Every distinct option a player in this state can be shown, gathered by
/// rolling the offer many times over.
fn every_option(current: AttackKind, level: u8) -> alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem> {
    every_option_on_wave(current, level, 1)
}

/// The same, on a stated wave. From [`RULE_OFFER_WAVE`] the shape of an offer
/// changes, and both shapes have to be reachable from a test.
fn every_option_on_wave(current: AttackKind, level: u8, wave: i64) -> alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem> {
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    let mut out: alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem> = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
    for seed in 1..=400u64 {
        let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
        let offer = Offer::roll(
            &v,
            0.0,
            ground,
            current,
            level,
            Boons::default(),
            wave,
            WaveRule::Normal,
            0,
            &mut rng,
        );
        for c in offer.choices.iter().take(offer.count) {
            if !out.contains(&c.item) {
                out.push(c.item);
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

#[test]
fn another_weapon_arrives_one_level_short_of_the_one_in_hand() {
    // A swap costs a single step rather than the whole climb. It used to cost
    // the climb, which meant a run that had reached the third level of
    // anything could not look at the roster again, and the second half of
    // every run played itself.
    for (held_level, expected) in [(1u8, 1u8), (2, 1), (3, 2)] {
        let seen = every_option(AttackKind::Hammer, held_level);
        let others: alloc::vec::Vec<(AttackKind, u8)> = seen
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|i| match i {
                OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } if *kind != AttackKind::Hammer => {
                    Some((*kind, *level))
                }
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        assert!(!others.is_empty(), "the roster never came up at level {held_level}");
        for (kind, level) in others {
            assert_eq!(
                level, expected,
                "holding a level {held_level} hammer, {kind:?} was offered at {level}"
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_weapon_in_hand_is_still_the_only_one_that_climbs() {
    // One short and not level: what is already carried should still be worth
    // having kept, so its own step stays a step ahead of any swap.
    for held_level in 1..MAX_LEVEL {
        let seen = every_option(AttackKind::Hammer, held_level);
        let own = seen
            .iter()
            .find_map(|i| match i {
                OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } if *kind == AttackKind::Hammer => Some(*level),
                _ => None,
            })
            .expect("its own next level should be on the table");
        assert_eq!(own, held_level + 1);
        assert!(own > held_level.saturating_sub(1).max(1), "a swap matched the climb");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_run_still_on_the_plain_swing_is_offered_plain_weapons() {
    // `Basic` has no level to discount from, and one short of one is one.
    let seen = every_option(AttackKind::Basic, 1);
    for item in &seen {
        if let OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } = item {
            assert_ne!(*kind, AttackKind::Basic, "the plain swing was offered back");
            assert_eq!(*level, 1, "{kind:?} should arrive fresh");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_mastered_weapon_stops_offering_its_own_next_level() {
    // There isn't one. What it does not stop offering is the roster, which is
    // the whole point of the discount.
    let seen = every_option(AttackKind::Hammer, MAX_LEVEL);
    assert!(
        !seen
            .iter()
            .any(|i| matches!(i, OfferItem::Attack { kind, .. } if *kind == AttackKind::Hammer)),
        "a mastered weapon was offered another level"
    );
    assert!(
        seen.iter()
            .any(|i| matches!(i, OfferItem::Attack { kind, .. } if *kind != AttackKind::Hammer)),
        "and the roster should still be there"
    );
}

#[test]
fn an_option_says_which_level_it_arrives_at() {
    // A fresh weapon at level two is a different offer from one at level one,
    // so the box has to say so. Only a plain level 1 goes unmarked.
    let ground = 0.0;
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let stand = |item| Offer::scripted(&v, 0.0, ground, &[item], 0).choices[0];

    let fresh = stand(OfferItem::Attack { kind: AttackKind::Tall, level: 1 });
    assert_eq!(fresh.label(AttackKind::Hammer), "HEAVY");

    let stepped = stand(OfferItem::Attack { kind: AttackKind::Tall, level: 2 });
    assert_eq!(stepped.label(AttackKind::Hammer), "HEAVY 2");

    let own = stand(OfferItem::Attack { kind: AttackKind::Hammer, level: 2 });
    assert_eq!(own.label(AttackKind::Hammer), "HAMMER 2");
}

#[test]
fn a_campaign_still_hands_its_own_weapon_over_at_level_one() {
    // Not a swap away from something else - it is the weapon the run was
    // started for arriving for the first time, so there is nothing to discount.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut rng = Rng::new(3);
    // Every boon already held, so the pool is the weapon and the wall it is
    // not carrying - small enough that both stand and neither can be shuffled
    // out of the three boxes.
    let held = Boons {
        double_jump: true,
        dash_free: true,
        shield: true,
        wall: WallMod::Pull,
    };
    let offer = Offer::roll_campaign(
        &v,
        0.0,
        v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT),
        AttackKind::Tall,
        AttackKind::Basic,
        1,
        held,
        0,
        &mut rng,
    );
    let weapon = offer
        .choices
        .iter()
        .take(offer.count)
        .find_map(|c| match c.item {
            OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } => Some((kind, level)),
            _ => None,
        })
        .expect("the campaign weapon should be on the table");
    assert_eq!(weapon, (AttackKind::Tall, 1));
}

/* ---------------- the late offer ---------------- */

#[test]
fn from_the_late_wave_two_of_the_three_boxes_are_terms() {
    // One upgrade still stands - the run should never stop offering anything
    // at all - and the other two are the terms the coming wave is fought under.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    for seed in 1..=200u64 {
        let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
        let offer = Offer::roll(
            &v,
            0.0,
            ground,
            AttackKind::Hammer,
            2,
            Boons::default(),
            RULE_OFFER_WAVE,
            WaveRule::Normal,
            0,
            &mut rng,
        );
        assert_eq!(offer.count, 3, "seed {seed}");
        let rules = offer
            .choices
            .iter()
            .take(offer.count)
            .filter(|c| matches!(c.item, OfferItem::Rule(_)))
            .count();
        assert_eq!(rules, 2, "seed {seed}: {} terms offered", rules);
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_wave_before_the_late_one_still_offers_three_upgrades() {
    // The line has to be exactly where it says it is.
    let seen = every_option_on_wave(AttackKind::Hammer, 2, RULE_OFFER_WAVE - 1);
    assert!(
        !seen.iter().any(|i| matches!(i, OfferItem::Rule(_))),
        "terms arrived a wave early"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_terms_on_offer_are_never_the_ones_the_wave_already_rolled() {
    // Taking it would be a box that changes nothing, and two of the three
    // boxes here are the only choice the player has left.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    for rolled in WaveRule::EVERY {
        for seed in 1..=120u64 {
            let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
            let offer = Offer::roll(
                &v,
                0.0,
                ground,
                AttackKind::Hammer,
                2,
                Boons::default(),
                RULE_OFFER_WAVE,
                rolled,
                0,
                &mut rng,
            );
            for c in offer.choices.iter().take(offer.count) {
                assert_ne!(
                    c.item,
                    OfferItem::Rule(rolled),
                    "{rolled:?} was offered on a wave already carrying it"
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_terms_on_offer_are_two_different_ones() {
    // Two boxes saying the same thing is one box and a wasted one.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    for seed in 1..=200u64 {
        let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
        let offer = Offer::roll(
            &v,
            0.0,
            ground,
            AttackKind::Hammer,
            2,
            Boons::default(),
            RULE_OFFER_WAVE,
            WaveRule::Normal,
            0,
            &mut rng,
        );
        let rules: alloc::vec::Vec<OfferItem> = offer
            .choices
            .iter()
            .take(offer.count)
            .filter(|c| matches!(c.item, OfferItem::Rule(_)))
            .map(|c| c.item)
            .collect();
        assert_ne!(rules[0], rules[1], "seed {seed}: the same term twice");
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_modifier_can_be_drawn_and_they_are_the_rules_not_the_kinds() {
    // Explicitly the wave's *rules* - what the player may do - and never its
    // kind, which is who turns up. The two are separate axes and only one of
    // them is being handed over.
    // Rolled from a wave already carrying BLIND, so the one rule left out of
    // the pool is BLIND and every other - the plain wave included - is
    // reachable.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    let mut drawn: alloc::vec::Vec<WaveRule> = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
    for seed in 1..=400u64 {
        let mut rng = Rng::new(seed);
        let offer = Offer::roll(
            &v,
            0.0,
            ground,
            AttackKind::Hammer,
            2,
            Boons::default(),
            RULE_OFFER_WAVE,
            WaveRule::Hidden,
            0,
            &mut rng,
        );
        for c in offer.choices.iter().take(offer.count) {
            if let OfferItem::Rule(rule) = c.item {
                if !drawn.contains(&rule) {
                    drawn.push(rule);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for rule in WaveRule::EVERY {
        if rule != WaveRule::Hidden {
            assert!(drawn.contains(&rule), "{rule:?} can never be drawn");
        }
    }
    assert!(
        drawn.contains(&WaveRule::Normal),
        "being left alone should be one of the things on offer"
    );
    // And nothing that belongs to the other axis leaked in. `WaveKind` has no
    // representation here at all, which is the strongest form of that.
    assert_eq!(drawn.len(), WaveRule::EVERY.len() - 1);
}

#[test]
fn taking_terms_sets_them_for_the_coming_wave_only() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.wave = RULE_OFFER_WAVE;
    // An offer only stands while the game is between waves; outside a lull it
    // is taken down before it can ever arm.
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    game.waves.rule = WaveRule::Normal;

    let v = game.viewport;
    let ground = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT);
    game.offer = Some(Offer::scripted(
        &v,
        game.camera_x,
        ground,
        &[OfferItem::Rule(WaveRule::Hidden)],
        game.timer,
    ));
    let body = game.offer.as_ref().unwrap().choices[0].body;
    wait_for_armed(&mut game);
    swing_at(&mut game, 0, body);
    game.tick(&idle());
    assert_eq!(game.waves.rule, WaveRule::Hidden, "the term was not applied");

    // A term is not a pin: the wave after this one rolls its own again.
    assert_eq!(game.waves.forced_rule, None, "it pinned the rest of the run");
    game.waves.begin_wave(game.wave + 1, &mut game.rng);
    assert!(
        game.waves.rule != WaveRule::Hidden || WaveRule::EVERY.len() == 1,
        "the next wave inherited the term"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_late_run_is_offered_the_roster_at_its_top_level() {
    // By then a swap has to be a real option inside a single wave: there is no
    // run left to spend climbing back up.
    let seen = every_option_on_wave(AttackKind::Hammer, 1, RULE_OFFER_WAVE);
    let others: alloc::vec::Vec<(AttackKind, u8)> = seen
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|i| match i {
            OfferItem::Attack { kind, level } if *kind != AttackKind::Hammer => {
                Some((*kind, *level))
            }
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect();
    assert!(!others.is_empty(), "the roster never came up");
    for (kind, level) in others {
        assert_eq!(level, MAX_LEVEL, "{kind:?} arrived at {level} late on");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_late_lull_offers_without_being_paid_for() {
    // Two of its three boxes are the terms of *this* wave and are worth
    // nothing a wave later, so they cannot wait on a score threshold.
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.wave = RULE_OFFER_WAVE;
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.score = 0;
    }
    assert!(game.total_score() < OFFER_SCORE_STEP, "the premise");
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_some(), "a late lull should offer anyway");
}

#[test]
fn an_earlier_lull_is_still_paid_for() {
    let mut game = new_game();
    game.start_run(1);
    settle_on_floor(&mut game);
    game.wave = RULE_OFFER_WAVE - 2;
    for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
        p.score = 0;
    }
    into_the_lull(&mut game);
    assert!(game.offer.is_none(), "an early offer has to be earned");
}

#[test]
fn a_term_box_says_it_is_only_for_this_wave() {
    // The one thing a player has to know about a modifier is that it is not
    // kept, unlike everything else a box has ever handed over.
    let game = new_game();
    let v = game.viewport;
    let stand = |item| Offer::scripted(&v, 0.0, 0.0, &[item], 0).choices[0];
    let blind = stand(OfferItem::Rule(WaveRule::Hidden));
    assert_eq!(blind.label(AttackKind::Basic), "BLIND");
    assert_eq!(blind.role(), "THIS WAVE");

    let plain = stand(OfferItem::Rule(WaveRule::Normal));
    assert_eq!(plain.label(AttackKind::Basic), "PLAIN", "the plain wave needs a word");
}

/// A shedder boss standing in reach, with health enough to be hit repeatedly.
fn plant_shedder_boss(game: &mut Game) -> Body {
    let v = game.viewport;
    let mut z = Zombie::shedder_boss(&v, &mut game.rng);
    z.body.y = v.hper(GROUND_Y_PCT) - z.body.h;
    z.body.x = game.players[0].body.x + v.wper(6.0);
    z.hp = 1_000_000.0;
    z.hpmax = 1_000_000.0;
    let body = z.body;
    game.zombies.push(z);
    body
}

/// Hits the shedder `times` over, chasing it down after each hit, and reports
/// how many husks it ended up standing.
fn husks_after_hits(game: &mut Game, times: usize) -> usize {
    for _ in 0..times {
        let body = the_shedder(game).body;
        // It is out of reach after every hit, so walk to it first.
        game.players[0].body.x = body.x - game.players[0].body.w;
        swing_at(game, 0, body);
        game.tick(&idle());
    }
    the_shedder(game).husks.len()
}

#[test]
fn a_walled_in_wave_caps_what_the_shedder_boss_leaves_behind() {
    // HOLD THE LINE stops the camera and walls the player into one screen. A
    // boss that keeps every husk it has ever left turns that into a room
    // filling up with no way out of it.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::StaticCamera);
    plant_shedder_boss(&mut game);
    assert_eq!(husks_after_hits(&mut game, 8), STATIC_CAMERA_HUSKS);
}

#[test]
fn anywhere_else_the_shedder_boss_still_keeps_every_husk() {
    // The arena is open on every other wave, so its husks are ground the
    // player gives up and walks away from.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::Normal);
    plant_shedder_boss(&mut game);
    let left = husks_after_hits(&mut game, 8);
    assert!(
        left > STATIC_CAMERA_HUSKS,
        "an open wave should not have capped it: {left}"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_cap_never_raises_what_an_ordinary_shedder_keeps() {
    // It already keeps fewer, and a cap is a ceiling rather than a quota.
    assert!(SHEDDER_HUSKS <= STATIC_CAMERA_HUSKS, "the cap is below the ordinary count");
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::StaticCamera);
    plant_shedder(&mut game);
    // Enough health to survive being hit five times; an ordinary one has not.
    if let Some(z) = game.zombies.iter_mut().find(|z| z.behaviors.shed) {
        z.hp = 1_000_000.0;
        z.hpmax = 1_000_000.0;
    }
    assert_eq!(husks_after_hits(&mut game, 5), SHEDDER_HUSKS);
}

#[test]
fn a_boss_already_over_the_cap_trims_down_to_it() {
    // The rule can be chosen in the lull from `RULE_OFFER_WAVE`, so a wave can
    // begin walled in with a boss that was not. It gives ground back on its
    // next shed rather than holding what it had.
    let mut game = game_with_rule(WaveRule::Normal);
    plant_shedder_boss(&mut game);
    let before = husks_after_hits(&mut game, 8);
    assert!(before > STATIC_CAMERA_HUSKS, "the premise: {before} husks standing");

    game.waves.rule = WaveRule::StaticCamera;
    assert_eq!(husks_after_hits(&mut game, 1), STATIC_CAMERA_HUSKS);
}


================================================================================
FILE: erect-core/src/waves.rs
================================================================================

//! Wave pacing and the weighted spawn tables.
//!
//! The browser version drove spawning from chained `setTimeout` calls running
//! independently of the render loop. Here it is a tick-driven state machine, so
//! everything advances on the same fixed clock as the simulation.

use crate::entities::Rng;

/// Which enemy variant to build, once the caller has decided "spawn a ground
/// enemy" or "spawn a flyer". Each entry unlocks at `min_wave` and is chosen by
/// weight among those currently unlocked, so new variants phase in gradually
/// instead of replacing the base roster.
pub struct SpawnEntry {
    pub weight: u32,
    pub min_wave: i64,
    pub kind: GroundKind,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum GroundKind {
    Base,
    Runt,
    Jumper,
    Leaper,
    Armored,
    Frenzied,
    Splitter,
    Blinker,
    Shooter,
    /// Blinks out of reach when hurt and leaves a standing hazard where it was.
    Shedder,
}

/// Which boss to put out. From [`BOSS_RAMP_FIRST_WAVE`] each one in a wave is
/// drawn separately, so a late boss wave is a mixed set rather than a row of
/// the same thing.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum BossKind {
    /// The white slab the game has always had.
    Ground,
    Flying,
    /// Blinks away when hurt and leaves a standing hazard.
    Shedder,
    /// A rolled combination at boss health, which makes its own crowd.
    Rolled,
}

impl BossKind {
    pub const ALL: [BossKind; 4] = [
        BossKind::Ground,
        BossKind::Flying,
        BossKind::Shedder,
        BossKind::Rolled,
    ];

    fn roll(rng: &mut Rng) -> Self {
        Self::ALL[rng.range(0, Self::ALL.len() as i32 - 1) as usize]
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum FlyerKind {
    Base,
    Teleporter,
}

pub const GROUND_SPAWN_TABLE: [SpawnEntry; 10] = [
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 6,
        min_wave: 1,
        kind: GroundKind::Base,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 2,
        kind: GroundKind::Runt,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 3,
        kind: GroundKind::Jumper,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 4,
        kind: GroundKind::Leaper,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 5,
        kind: GroundKind::Armored,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 6,
        kind: GroundKind::Frenzied,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 7,
        kind: GroundKind::Splitter,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 8,
        kind: GroundKind::Blinker,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 9,
        kind: GroundKind::Shooter,
    },
    SpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: crate::config::SHEDDER_MIN_WAVE,
        kind: GroundKind::Shedder,
    },
];

pub struct FlyerSpawnEntry {
    pub weight: u32,
    pub min_wave: i64,
    pub kind: FlyerKind,
}

pub const FLYER_SPAWN_TABLE: [FlyerSpawnEntry; 2] = [
    FlyerSpawnEntry {
        weight: 3,
        min_wave: 1,
        kind: FlyerKind::Base,
    },
    FlyerSpawnEntry {
        weight: 2,
        min_wave: 4,
        kind: FlyerKind::Teleporter,
    },
];

pub fn pick_ground(wave: i64, rng: &mut Rng) -> GroundKind {
    let total: u32 = GROUND_SPAWN_TABLE
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| wave >= e.min_wave)
        .map(|e| e.weight)
        .sum();
    let mut roll = rng.unit() * total as f32;
    for entry in GROUND_SPAWN_TABLE.iter().filter(|e| wave >= e.min_wave) {
        roll -= entry.weight as f32;
        if roll < 0.0 {
            return entry.kind;
        }
    }
    GroundKind::Base
}

pub fn pick_flyer(wave: i64, rng: &mut Rng) -> FlyerKind {
    let total: u32 = FLYER_SPAWN_TABLE
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| wave >= e.min_wave)
        .map(|e| e.weight)
        .sum();
    let mut roll = rng.unit() * total as f32;
    for entry in FLYER_SPAWN_TABLE.iter().filter(|e| wave >= e.min_wave) {
        roll -= entry.weight as f32;
        if roll < 0.0 {
            return entry.kind;
        }
    }
    FlyerKind::Base
}

/// Occasionally a wave is restricted to one kind of enemy, which changes how it
/// has to be fought: an all-flyer wave cannot be meleed the usual way, an
/// all-jumper wave never leaves the ground clear.
///
/// A restricted wave ignores the usual `min_wave` gating - that is the point of
/// it. A jumpers-only wave can turn up before jumpers would normally appear.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WaveKind {
    /// The ordinary weighted mix.
    Mixed,
    /// Ground enemies only, any variant.
    GroundOnly,
    FlyersOnly,
    /// Nothing but plain zombies.
    BasicOnly,
    JumpersOnly,
}

/// How a wave plays, as opposed to who turns up in it.
///
/// A separate axis from [`WaveKind`] on purpose: one enum holding both would
/// make the two mutually exclusive, and "jumpers only, and you cannot jump" is
/// exactly the sort of pairing worth having. At most one of each per wave.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WaveRule {
    Normal,
    /// The view stops where the wave began and the player is walled in. Ground
    /// enemies walk to them and flyers are held in view anyway, so only the
    /// player needs the walls.
    StaticCamera,
    /// No jumping. Down puts the wall up from standing, and the attack box is
    /// twice as long to make up for the lost mobility.
    NoJumps,
    /// No wall. The slam button instead buys invulnerability in mid-air, and
    /// landing heals a quarter of full health.
    NoWall,
    /// Nothing visible but the player, its attacks, blasts and score popups.
    /// Almost harmless, since fighting blind otherwise is not a fight.
    Hidden,
    /// The ground gives no traction. Standing on it, the player can turn to
    /// face either way but cannot walk anywhere; every step across the arena
    /// has to be jumped. The dash still works, so there is a way off a bad
    /// piece of floor that does not need height.
    ///
    /// Named for what it takes away rather than for what it leaves, because
    /// the wave *kind* beside it may already be reading GROUND ONLY - which is
    /// about who spawns, not about how the player moves. "GROUND ONLY /
    /// AIRBORNE" on one screen reads as a contradiction; "GROUND ONLY / NO
    /// FOOTING" reads as two facts.
    NoFooting,
}

impl WaveRule {
    const CHANCE: f32 = 0.25;

    /// Every modifier a wave can carry, the plain one included.
    ///
    /// The order is the offer's, not the roll's - the roll picks by number and
    /// must not be rewired to walk this. `Normal` is a member because from
    /// [`crate::config::RULE_OFFER_WAVE`] "leave the wave alone" is itself one
    /// of the things a box can offer.
    pub const EVERY: [WaveRule; 6] = [
        WaveRule::Normal,
        WaveRule::StaticCamera,
        WaveRule::NoJumps,
        WaveRule::NoWall,
        WaveRule::Hidden,
        WaveRule::NoFooting,
    ];

    pub fn label(self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        match self {
            WaveRule::Normal => None,
            WaveRule::StaticCamera => Some("HOLD THE LINE"),
            WaveRule::NoJumps => Some("GROUNDED"),
            WaveRule::NoWall => Some("NO WALL"),
            WaveRule::Hidden => Some("BLIND"),
            WaveRule::NoFooting => Some("NO FOOTING"),
        }
    }

    /// How much longer the attack box is on this wave.
    pub fn gun_reach(self) -> f32 {
        match self {
            WaveRule::NoJumps => 2.0,
            _ => 1.0,
        }
    }

    /// What enemy damage is multiplied by.
    pub fn damage_scale(self) -> f32 {
        match self {
            WaveRule::Hidden => 0.01,
            _ => 1.0,
        }
    }

    fn roll(wave: i64, rng: &mut Rng) -> Self {
        // The first wave is always plain, the same as for enemy kinds: the
        // opening should teach the game, not a variant of it.
        if wave <= 1 || rng.unit() >= Self::CHANCE {
            return WaveRule::Normal;
        }
        // `range` is inclusive at both ends, so five variants need 0..=4. Get
        // this wrong low and the catch-all swallows two values, and the last
        // variant comes up twice as often as the rest; get it wrong high and
        // one of them never comes up at all.
        match rng.range(0, 4) {
            0 => WaveRule::StaticCamera,
            1 => WaveRule::NoJumps,
            2 => WaveRule::NoWall,
            3 => WaveRule::Hidden,
            _ => WaveRule::NoFooting,
        }
    }
}

impl WaveKind {
    /// One in four waves is restricted.
    const SPECIAL_CHANCE: f32 = 0.25;

    /// Shown during the countdown so the player can see it coming.
    pub fn label(self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        match self {
            WaveKind::Mixed => None,
            WaveKind::GroundOnly => Some("GROUND ONLY"),
            WaveKind::FlyersOnly => Some("FLYERS ONLY"),
            WaveKind::BasicOnly => Some("HORDE"),
            WaveKind::JumpersOnly => Some("JUMPERS"),
        }
    }

    /// Rolls the kind for an upcoming wave.
    ///
    /// Wave 1 and boss waves stay mixed: the first wave is the introduction,
    /// and a boss wave already is the special one.
    pub fn roll(wave: i64, rng: &mut Rng) -> Self {
        if wave <= 1 || wave % 5 == 0 || rng.unit() >= Self::SPECIAL_CHANCE {
            return WaveKind::Mixed;
        }
        // Inclusive range: four variants need 0..=3, not 0..=4.
        match rng.range(0, 3) {
            0 => WaveKind::GroundOnly,
            1 => WaveKind::FlyersOnly,
            2 => WaveKind::BasicOnly,
            _ => WaveKind::JumpersOnly,
        }
    }
}

/// What the wave manager wants the game to do this tick.
// Not `Copy`: a late boss wave carries a list of what it drew.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum WaveAction {
    Idle,
    SpawnGround(GroundKind),
    SpawnFlyer(FlyerKind),
    SpawnBosses(i64),
    /// Wave 10's single flying boss, in place of that wave's ground bosses.
    SpawnFlyingBoss,
    /// Wave 15's alternative: one shedder boss instead of that wave's three
    /// ground bosses. Rolled, so the wave is not the same fight every run.
    SpawnShedderBoss,
    /// A late boss wave's mixed set, each drawn separately.
    SpawnBossGroup(alloc::vec::Vec<BossKind>),
    /// Wave 20's whole content: one rolled boss and nothing else at all.
    SpawnRolledBoss,
    /// This many rolled heavies at once, from [`ELITE_FIRST_WAVE`] on. What
    /// they turn out to be is the caller's business - the manager only decides
    /// how many and when.
    SpawnElite(usize),
    ClearWave,
}

pub struct WaveManager {
    /// What kind of wave is running. Rolled when a wave begins.
    pub kind: WaveKind,
    pub rule: WaveRule,
    /// Pinned by the developer menu. `None` leaves the roll alone, which is
    /// what every ordinary run uses.
    pub forced_kind: Option<WaveKind>,
    pub forced_rule: Option<WaveRule>,
    boss_due: bool,
    /// Rolled heavies this wave still owes, and how many come out together.
    ///
    /// A count rather than a flag: past [`ELITE_RAMP_FIRST_WAVE`] a wave is
    /// made of them, and they have to be spread through it rather than all
    /// turning up at one mark.
    elites_left: usize,
    elite_group: usize,
    /// -1 when no countdown is running, otherwise the number still displayed.
    pub countdown: i32,
    countdown_timer: i32,
    spawn_timer: i32,
}

impl Default for WaveManager {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            kind: WaveKind::Mixed,
            rule: WaveRule::Normal,
            forced_kind: None,
            forced_rule: None,
            boss_due: true,
            elites_left: 1,
            elite_group: 1,
            countdown: -1,
            countdown_timer: 0,
            spawn_timer: 0,
        }
    }
}

impl WaveManager {
    /// Back to a fresh run. The pinned modifiers survive on purpose: they are
    /// set before the run starts and are meant to hold for all of it.
    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        let (kind, rule) = (self.forced_kind, self.forced_rule);
        *self = Self::default();
        self.forced_kind = kind;
        self.forced_rule = rule;
    }

    /// Ends the lull immediately; the next wave starts spawning at once.
    pub fn skip_countdown(&mut self) {
        self.countdown = -1;
        self.countdown_timer = 0;
    }

    /// True while the game is between waves.
    pub fn between_waves(&self) -> bool {
        self.countdown >= 0
    }

    pub fn begin_countdown(&mut self, seconds: i32) {
        self.countdown = seconds;
        self.countdown_timer = crate::config::TICKS_PER_SEC as i32;
    }

    /// Decides what the upcoming wave will be made of.
    pub fn begin_wave(&mut self, wave: i64, rng: &mut Rng) {
        // Both rolls happen either way, pinned or not, so that pinning one does
        // not shift the stream the rest of the run draws from.
        let (kind, rule) = (WaveKind::roll(wave, rng), WaveRule::roll(wave, rng));
        self.kind = self.forced_kind.unwrap_or(kind);
        self.rule = self.forced_rule.unwrap_or(rule);
        self.elites_left = crate::config::elites_in_wave(wave);
        self.elite_group = crate::config::elite_group_size(wave);
    }

    /// The spawn count the next heavy is due at.
    ///
    /// The arrivals are spread evenly across the wave rather than counted off
    /// from the start: a wave owing seven of them puts one at every seventh of
    /// its budget. The first is a whole interval in, so the wave always opens
    /// as an ordinary one - which is the same reason a single heavy waited for
    /// a quarter of the budget before this ramp existed.
    fn next_elite_at(&self, wave: i64) -> i64 {
        let budget = crate::config::wave_budget(wave);
        let total = crate::config::elites_in_wave(wave);
        let group = crate::config::elite_group_size(wave).max(1);
        // Arrivals, not heavies: a pair is one arrival and takes one slot.
        let arrivals = total.div_ceil(group).max(1) as i64;
        if arrivals == 1 {
            // Nothing to space out. It still waits, for the same reason one
            // ever did.
            return budget / crate::config::ELITE_ENTRY_FRACTION;
        }
        let done = (total - self.elites_left).div_ceil(group) as i64;
        (budget * (done + 1)) / (arrivals + 1)
    }

    /// Advances pacing by one tick and reports what should happen.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn update(
        &mut self,
        wave: i64,
        spawn_count: i64,
        live_enemies: usize,
        rng: &mut Rng,
    ) -> WaveAction {
        if self.countdown >= 0 {
            self.countdown_timer -= 1;
            if self.countdown_timer <= 0 {
                self.countdown -= 1;
                self.countdown_timer = crate::config::TICKS_PER_SEC as i32;
            }
            return WaveAction::Idle;
        }

        if self.spawn_timer > 0 {
            self.spawn_timer -= 1;
            return WaveAction::Idle;
        }

        // The wave's heavies, spread through it. Boss waves are skipped: three
        // bosses and a rolled heavy in one wave is two headlines reading over
        // each other.
        if self.elites_left > 0
            && wave >= crate::config::ELITE_FIRST_WAVE
            && wave % 5 != 0
            && spawn_count >= self.next_elite_at(wave)
        {
            let group = self.elite_group.min(self.elites_left);
            self.elites_left -= group;
            self.spawn_timer = rng.range(6, 60);
            return WaveAction::SpawnElite(group);
        }

        // A wave that spawns nothing ordinary has spent its budget the moment
        // its boss is out; there is no count left to work through. Without this
        // the wave would sit forever a hundred and fifty short of a budget it
        // was never going to spend.
        if wave == crate::config::ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE && !self.boss_due {
            return if live_enemies == 0 {
                WaveAction::ClearWave
            } else {
                self.spawn_timer = 18;
                WaveAction::Idle
            };
        }

        if spawn_count < crate::config::wave_budget(wave) {
            // Boss waves drop their whole group in one go, once per wave.
            if wave % 5 == 0 {
                if self.boss_due {
                    self.boss_due = false;
                    self.spawn_timer = rng.range(6, 60); // 100-1000 ms at 60 Hz
                    // One wave belongs to the flying boss, and it comes alone;
                    // every other boss wave keeps its ground bosses.
                    return if wave >= crate::config::BOSS_RAMP_FIRST_WAVE {
                        // Past the ramp a boss wave is a count and a handful of
                        // draws rather than a set piece.
                        let n = crate::config::bosses_in_wave(wave);
                        WaveAction::SpawnBossGroup(
                            (0..n).map(|_| BossKind::roll(rng)).collect(),
                        )
                    } else if wave == crate::config::ROLLED_BOSS_WAVE {
                        WaveAction::SpawnRolledBoss
                    } else if wave == crate::config::FLYING_BOSS_WAVE {
                        WaveAction::SpawnFlyingBoss
                    } else if wave == crate::config::SHEDDER_BOSS_WAVE && rng.flip() {
                        // A coin flip rather than a fixed owner: wave 10 always
                        // belongs to the flying boss, but this wave is worth
                        // being two different fights.
                        WaveAction::SpawnShedderBoss
                    } else {
                        WaveAction::SpawnBosses(wave / 5)
                    };
                }
            } else {
                self.boss_due = true;
            }

            self.spawn_timer = rng.range(6, 60);

            if live_enemies < crate::config::max_concurrent_enemies(wave) {
                return match self.kind {
                    // A restricted wave spawns on every opportunity; the coin
                    // flips below are what make a mixed wave feel sparse, and
                    // applying them here would make special waves feel empty.
                    WaveKind::GroundOnly => WaveAction::SpawnGround(pick_ground(wave, rng)),
                    WaveKind::FlyersOnly => WaveAction::SpawnFlyer(pick_flyer(wave, rng)),
                    WaveKind::BasicOnly => WaveAction::SpawnGround(GroundKind::Base),
                    WaveKind::JumpersOnly => WaveAction::SpawnGround(if rng.flip() {
                        GroundKind::Jumper
                    } else {
                        GroundKind::Leaper
                    }),
                    WaveKind::Mixed => {
                        if rng.flip() {
                            WaveAction::SpawnGround(pick_ground(wave, rng))
                        } else if rng.flip() {
                            WaveAction::SpawnFlyer(pick_flyer(wave, rng))
                        } else {
                            WaveAction::Idle
                        }
                    }
                };
            }
            return WaveAction::Idle;
        }

        if live_enemies == 0 {
            return WaveAction::ClearWave;
        }

        // Budget spent but stragglers remain: check back shortly.
        self.spawn_timer = 18;
        WaveAction::Idle
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: erect-desktop/build.rs
================================================================================

//! Turns one confusing failure into an instruction.
//!
//! `cargo psp` run from the repository root builds the whole workspace for
//! `mipsel-sony-psp` - this crate included, which cannot work: it is macroquad,
//! cpal and gilrs, none of which know what a PSP is. What the developer sees
//! without this is `gilrs` panicking inside its own build script on a missing
//! `CARGO_CFG_TARGET_FAMILY`, which is true (the PSP target has no family) and
//! says nothing at all about the actual mistake.
//!
//! The PSP frontend is a separate workspace for exactly this reason and has to
//! be built from its own directory.

fn main() {
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=build.rs");
    if std::env::var("CARGO_CFG_TARGET_OS").as_deref() == Ok("psp") {
        panic!(
            "\n\n\
             erect-desktop cannot be built for the PSP.\n\n\
             This usually means `cargo psp` was run from the repository root,\n\
             where it tries to build every crate in the workspace for the PSP.\n\
             The PSP frontend is its own workspace - build it from there:\n\n    \
             cd erect-psp && cargo psp --release\n\n"
        );
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: erect-desktop/Cargo.toml

[package]
name = "erect-desktop"
description = "Desktop frontend for E-Rect: macroquad rendering, keyboard and gamepads"
version.workspace = true
edition.workspace = true

[[bin]]
name = "erect"
path = "src/main.rs"

[features]
# An unattended run that saves what it drew. Never in a shipped build: it exists
# so a change to the renderer can be checked without a person at the keyboard.
harness = []

[dependencies]
erect-core = { path = "../erect-core" }
erect-audio = { path = "../erect-audio" }
# 2D rendering, windowing, keyboard and touch. No system libraries on
# Windows/macOS, only the usual X11/GL ones on Linux - and it targets
# wasm32-unknown-unknown, which is what makes the browser build possible at all.
macroquad = "0.4"
# The save format, on every platform. A browser has no filesystem but it has
# localStorage, and the same JSON goes into both.
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1"

# Everything below wants an operating system. A browser has none of it: no
# device enumeration, no gamepad subsystem, no filesystem. The build is silent
# there and the settings live only as long as the tab does.
[target.'cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))'.dependencies]
# Cross-platform gamepads: XInput on Windows, evdev on Linux, IOKit on macOS.
gilrs = "0.11"
# Per-OS config directory for the settings file.
directories = "5"
# Own mixer + raw device access: six layers must stay sample-locked, which
# per-source playback APIs cannot guarantee.
cpal = "0.15"
claxon = "0.4"

FILE: erect-desktop/src/harness.rs

//! Runs the game unattended and saves what it drew.
//!
//! Verifying that something *renders* is the one thing the test suite cannot
//! do: it exercises the core, and a marker that never reaches the screen looks
//! identical from there. Driving a browser by synthetic clicks was the other
//! option and it lost most of them; a native build with a fixed seed, a fixed
//! frame count and a file on disk is repeatable.
//!
//! Off unless `ERECT_HARNESS` is set, so an ordinary run never notices.

use erect_core::dev::DevSetup;
use erect_core::game::Game;
use erect_core::input::InputFrame;
use macroquad::prelude::*;

pub struct Harness {
    /// Frames to draw before saving. Enough to let the wave spawn.
    frames: u32,
    shots: Vec<(u32, String)>,
    /// Saved on the first frame a rolled heavy is being announced. A fixed
    /// frame number cannot catch that: when it arrives depends on how fast the
    /// wave spent its budget.
    on_elite: Option<String>,
    /// Frames to wait after a heavy is announced before saving.
    ///
    /// Enemies are placed just outside the view and walk in, so the frame a
    /// heavy is announced on is the one frame it is guaranteed *not* to be
    /// visible in. Three attempts at photographing one went that way.
    on_elite_delay: u32,
    on_elite_at: Option<u32>,
    /// Saves the frame the between-wave offer is standing, which is the only
    /// time its three options are on screen.
    on_offer: Option<String>,
    /// Saves the frame a blind wave lets its enemies show, and the frame after
    /// it does not. Frame numbers cannot be used for this: they are not ticks.
    on_glimpse: Option<String>,
    after_glimpse: Option<String>,
    /// Frame the "hidden" companion shot is due on. Taken a few frames later
    /// than the glimpse rather than the very next one: reading the framebuffer
    /// gives back the frame that was last presented, which is one behind.
    after_glimpse_at: Option<u32>,
    /// Not before this frame. A blind wave shows its enemies on tick zero, when
    /// there are none yet to show.
    glimpse_after: u32,
    on_offer_delay: u32,
    on_offer_at: Option<u32>,
    /// Keeps the players standing. Nothing drives them here, so an ordinary run
    /// ends within a wave or two - long before the thing under test arrives.
    keep_alive: bool,
    /// Swings on a fixed rhythm and turns to face whatever is nearest.
    ///
    /// Not a good player - a working one. A wave only spawns as fast as the
    /// field is cleared, so a run that never kills anything stalls at the crowd
    /// limit and the later half of the wave, heavy included, never arrives.
    fight: bool,
    /// Raises a wall every so often, so one can be looked at.
    wall: bool,
    /// Presses attack once, on the first frame, and then nothing.
    ///
    /// `fight` swings every twelve ticks, which for a placed box means it is
    /// always a fresh one. Watching a box age needs exactly one press.
    once: bool,
}

impl Harness {
    /// `ERECT_HARNESS=wave=7,alive,fight,frames=600,out=/tmp/a.png`
    ///
    /// Flags may be bare or written `=1`; anything unrecognised is ignored, so
    /// a stale spelling is a missing feature rather than a failure to start.
    pub fn from_env(game: &mut Game) -> Option<Self> {
        let spec = std::env::var("ERECT_HARNESS").ok()?;
        let mut dev = DevSetup::default();
        let mut frames = 600;
        let mut shots = Vec::new();
        let mut on_elite = None;
        let mut on_elite_delay = 0u32;
        let mut on_offer = None;
        let mut on_glimpse = None;
        let mut glimpse_after = 0u32;
        let mut on_offer_delay = 0u32;
        let mut keep_alive = false;
        let mut fight = false;
        let mut boons = false;
        let mut screen: Option<String> = None;
        let mut once = false;
        let mut lull = false;
        let mut written: Option<(erect_core::campaign::ModeKind, erect_core::attack::AttackKind)> =
            None;
        let mut wall = erect_core::boon::WallMod::Plain;
        for part in spec.split(',') {
            let (key, value) = part.split_once('=').unwrap_or((part, ""));
            match key {
                "wave" => dev.wave = value.parse().unwrap_or(1),
                "score" => dev.score = value.parse().unwrap_or(0),
                "rule" => {
                    dev.rule = match value {
                        "blind" => Some(erect_core::waves::WaveRule::Hidden),
                        "nofooting" => Some(erect_core::waves::WaveRule::NoFooting),
                        _ => None,
                    }
                }
                // `written=tutorial:hammer` - a real written run rather than a
                // dev one, so the mode can be watched from the outside as it
                // is actually played.
                "written" => {
                    if let Some((mode, weapon)) = value.split_once(':') {
                        let kind = match mode {
                            "campaign" => erect_core::campaign::ModeKind::Campaign,
                            _ => erect_core::campaign::ModeKind::Tutorial,
                        };
                        let weapon = erect_core::attack::AttackKind::EVERY
                            .iter()
                            .copied()
                            .find(|k| k.label().eq_ignore_ascii_case(weapon))
                            .unwrap_or(erect_core::attack::AttackKind::Basic);
                        written = Some((kind, weapon));
                    }
                }
                "once" => once = value != "0",
                // Opens in the between-wave countdown, which is the only time
                // the wave's modifiers are announced on screen.
                "lull" => lull = value != "0",
                "level" => dev.attack_level = value.parse().unwrap_or(1),
                // `arm=trap` - what a dev run starts holding, by the label the
                // offer screen shows.
                "arm" => {
                    if let Some(kind) = erect_core::attack::AttackKind::EVERY
                        .iter()
                        .copied()
                        .find(|k| k.label().eq_ignore_ascii_case(value))
                    {
                        dev.attack = kind;
                    }
                }
                "frames" => frames = value.parse().unwrap_or(600),
                "out" => shots.push((frames, value.to_string())),
                // A second and later snapshot, so one run can show a thing
                // arriving and the same thing being fought.
                "at" => {
                    if let Some((at, path)) = value.split_once(':') {
                        shots.push((at.parse().unwrap_or(0), path.to_string()));
                    }
                }
                "on_elite" => match value.split_once(':') {
                    Some((delay, path)) => {
                        on_elite_delay = delay.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                        on_elite = Some(path.to_string());
                    }
                    None => on_elite = Some(value.to_string()),
                },
                "on_glimpse" => match value.split_once(':') {
                    Some((from, path)) => {
                        glimpse_after = from.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                        on_glimpse = Some(path.to_string());
                    }
                    None => on_glimpse = Some(value.to_string()),
                },
                "on_offer" => match value.split_once(':') {
                    Some((delay, path)) => {
                        on_offer_delay = delay.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                        on_offer = Some(path.to_string());
                    }
                    None => on_offer = Some(value.to_string()),
                },
                "alive" => keep_alive = true,
                "fight" => fight = true,
                "boons" => boons = true,
                // Show a menu instead of playing, so a layout can be looked at.
                "screen" => screen = Some(value.to_string()),
                "wall" => wall = match value {
                    "pull" => erect_core::boon::WallMod::Pull,
                    "push" => erect_core::boon::WallMod::Push,
                    _ => erect_core::boon::WallMod::Plain,
                },
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        game.dev = dev;
        match screen.as_deref() {
            Some("dev") => game.state = erect_core::game::State::DevMenu,
            Some("title") => game.state = erect_core::game::State::Title,
            // `campaigns` opens on the tutorial, `campaigns2` on the campaign:
            // the screen changes its heading with the mode, so both halves of
            // it have to be capturable.
            Some("campaigns") => game.state = erect_core::game::State::Campaigns,
            Some("campaigns2") => {
                game.state = erect_core::game::State::Campaigns;
                game.campaign_mode = 1;
            }
            _ => match written {
                Some((kind, weapon)) => {
                    game.start_written(kind, weapon, 1);
                    // `score=` still applies, so a campaign lull can be reached
                    // without playing the twenty minutes it would cost to earn
                    // the first one.
                    if let Some(p) = game.players.first_mut() {
                        p.score = dev.score;
                    }
                    // And `level=` seeds the weapon, so the end of a campaign
                    // can be photographed without earning every offer between
                    // here and there.
                    if dev.attack_level > 1 {
                        for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
                            p.attack = weapon;
                            p.attack_level = dev.attack_level;
                        }
                    }
                }
                None => game.start_dev_run(),
            },
        }
        if lull {
            game.waves.begin_countdown(erect_core::config::WAVE_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS);
            // And stand what that lull would be offering. Rolled the same way
            // the core rolls it, because the point of the flag is to look at
            // the real thing: reaching wave twenty-six by playing is not
            // something this harness's fighter can do.
            if let Some(p) = game.players.first() {
                let (kind, level, held) = (p.attack, p.attack_level, p.boons);
                let v = game.viewport;
                let ground = v.hper(erect_core::config::GROUND_Y_PCT);
                let mut rng = erect_core::entities::Rng::new(dev.score.max(1) as u64);
                game.offer = Some(erect_core::offer::Offer::roll(
                    &v,
                    game.camera_x,
                    ground,
                    kind,
                    level,
                    held,
                    game.wave,
                    game.waves.rule,
                    game.timer,
                    &mut rng,
                ));
            }
        }
        shots.sort_by_key(|(at, _)| *at);
        if boons || wall != erect_core::boon::WallMod::Plain {
            for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
                p.boons = erect_core::boon::Boons {
                    double_jump: boons,
                    dash_free: boons,
                    shield: boons,
                    wall,
                };
                // A wall to actually raise, since nothing here earns one.
                p.super_charges = 9;
            }
        }
        Some(Self {
            frames,
            shots,
            on_elite,
            on_elite_delay,
            on_elite_at: None,
            on_offer,
            after_glimpse: on_glimpse.as_ref().map(|p| format!("{p}.dark.png")),
            after_glimpse_at: None,
            on_glimpse,
            glimpse_after,
            on_offer_delay,
            on_offer_at: None,
            keep_alive,
            fight,
            wall: wall != erect_core::boon::WallMod::Plain,
            once,
        })
    }

    /// True when the run is over and the process should stop.
    /// Fills in what a player would be doing.
    pub fn drive(&self, frame: &mut InputFrame, game: &Game, tick: u32) {
        // Jump, then slam: the wall needs the player off the floor.
        if self.wall {
            if let Some(me) = game.players.first() {
                frame.players[0].jump = me.grounded && tick % 90 == 0;
                frame.players[0].slam = !me.grounded && tick % 90 == 6;
            }
        }
        if self.once {
            frame.players[0].attack = tick == 1;
            return;
        }
        if !self.fight {
            return;
        }
        let Some(me) = game.players.first() else {
            return;
        };
        let nearest = game
            .zombies
            .iter()
            .map(|z| z.body.x)
            .chain(game.flyers.iter().map(|f| f.body.x))
            .min_by(|a, b| {
                (a - me.body.x)
                    .abs()
                    .partial_cmp(&(b - me.body.x).abs())
                    .unwrap()
            });
        let intent = &mut frame.players[0];
        if let Some(x) = nearest {
            let away = x - me.body.x;
            intent.right = away > 0.0;
            intent.left = away < 0.0;
        }
        // A swing every few ticks: held down, most kinds would only swing once.
        intent.attack = tick % 12 == 0;
        intent.attack_held = true;
    }

    /// Before the tick, so the players never reach a frame dead.
    pub fn before_tick(&self, game: &mut Game) {
        if self.wall {
            for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
                p.super_charges = p.super_charges.max(1);
            }
        }
        if self.keep_alive {
            for p in game.players.iter_mut() {
                p.hp = p.hpmax;
                p.dead = false;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn after_frame(&mut self, frame: u32, game: &Game) -> bool {
        if self.on_glimpse.is_some() && frame >= self.glimpse_after && game.enemies_visible() {
            let path = self.on_glimpse.take().unwrap();
            get_screen_data().export_png(&path);
            println!("harness: frame {frame} (enemies showing) -> {path}");
            self.after_glimpse_at = Some(frame + 5);
        }
        if self.after_glimpse_at.is_some_and(|due| frame >= due) && !game.enemies_visible() {
            self.after_glimpse_at = None;
            if let Some(path) = self.after_glimpse.take() {
                get_screen_data().export_png(&path);
                println!("harness: frame {frame} (enemies hidden) -> {path}");
            }
        }
        if self.on_offer.is_some() {
            if self.on_offer_at.is_none() && game.offer.is_some() {
                self.on_offer_at = Some(frame + self.on_offer_delay);
            }
            if self.on_offer_at.is_some_and(|due| frame >= due) {
                let path = self.on_offer.take().unwrap();
                get_screen_data().export_png(&path);
                println!("harness: frame {frame} (offer standing) -> {path}");
            }
        }
        if self.on_elite.is_some() {
            if self.on_elite_at.is_none() && game.elite_notice().is_some() {
                self.on_elite_at = Some(frame + self.on_elite_delay);
            }
            if self.on_elite_at.is_some_and(|due| frame >= due) {
                let path = self.on_elite.take().unwrap();
                get_screen_data().export_png(&path);
                println!("harness: frame {frame} (heavy, +{} frames) -> {path}", self.on_elite_delay);
            }
        }
        while let Some((at, path)) = self.shots.first() {
            if frame < *at {
                break;
            }
            // Straight off the framebuffer, so it is exactly what a player
            // would be looking at - pad, letterboxing and all.
            get_screen_data().export_png(path);
            println!("harness: frame {frame} -> {path}");
            self.shots.remove(0);
        }
        // The frame cap ends the run whatever happened. An event that never
        // fires is a result to report, not a reason to sit there drawing.
        if frame < self.frames {
            return false;
        }
        if let Some(path) = self.on_elite.take() {
            println!("harness: no heavy was ever announced, {path} not written");
        }
        if let Some(path) = self.on_offer.take() {
            println!("harness: no offer ever stood, {path} not written");
        }
        true
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: erect-desktop/src/input.rs
================================================================================

//! Keyboard and gamepad, collapsed into a per-frame snapshot of intents.
//!
//! The browser version was event-driven (keydown/keyup). macroquad and gilrs are
//! both polled, so instead of dispatching events we build one `InputFrame` per
//! rendered frame: `held` for movement, `pressed` for one-shot actions.

use erect_core::config::MAX_PLAYERS;
// Only the physical-pad path reads the deadzone, and a browser has no pads.
#[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
use erect_core::config::GAMEPAD_DEADZONE;
use erect_core::input::{InputFrame, MenuIntent, PlayerIntent};
use erect_core::settings::SchemeInfo;
#[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
use gilrs::{Axis, Button, Gilrs};
use macroquad::prelude::*;

/// What this platform offers. Index order is what gets stored in settings.
pub static SCHEMES: [SchemeInfo; 4] = [
    SchemeInfo { label: "W A S D + SPACE", is_gamepad: false, pad_index: 0 },
    SchemeInfo { label: "ARROWS + ENTER", is_gamepad: false, pad_index: 0 },
    SchemeInfo { label: "GAMEPAD 1", is_gamepad: true, pad_index: 0 },
    SchemeInfo { label: "GAMEPAD 2", is_gamepad: true, pad_index: 1 },
];

const SCHEME_KB_WASD: usize = 0;

/// Level state of one gamepad, so edges can be derived frame to frame.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
struct PadState {
    left: bool,
    right: bool,
    up: bool,
    down: bool,
    jump: bool,
    attack: bool,
    back: bool,
    pause: bool,
    /// The shoulder button, kept apart from the face buttons: it is the only
    /// pad input the game asks for that is not already spoken for.
    dash: bool,
}

pub struct InputReader {
    /// Absent in a browser: there is no gamepad subsystem to ask, and the pad
    /// on screen is what serves that role there.
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
    gilrs: Option<Gilrs>,
    prev: [PadState; MAX_PLAYERS],
    cur: [PadState; MAX_PLAYERS],
    connected: [bool; MAX_PLAYERS],
}

impl InputReader {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            // A missing gamepad subsystem must not stop the game from running.
            #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
            gilrs: Gilrs::new().ok(),
            prev: [PadState::default(); MAX_PLAYERS],
            cur: [PadState::default(); MAX_PLAYERS],
            connected: [false; MAX_PLAYERS],
        }
    }

    fn poll_pads(&mut self) {
        self.prev = self.cur;
        self.cur = [PadState::default(); MAX_PLAYERS];
        self.connected = [false; MAX_PLAYERS];
        #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
        self.poll_gilrs();
    }

    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
    fn poll_gilrs(&mut self) {
        let Some(gilrs) = self.gilrs.as_mut() else {
            return;
        };
        // Drain the event queue so gilrs keeps its internal state current.
        while gilrs.next_event().is_some() {}

        for (slot, (_id, pad)) in gilrs.gamepads().enumerate() {
            if slot >= MAX_PLAYERS {
                break;
            }
            self.connected[slot] = true;
            let axis_x = pad.value(Axis::LeftStickX);
            let axis_y = pad.value(Axis::LeftStickY);
            self.cur[slot] = PadState {
                left: axis_x < -GAMEPAD_DEADZONE || pad.is_pressed(Button::DPadLeft),
                right: axis_x > GAMEPAD_DEADZONE || pad.is_pressed(Button::DPadRight),
                // gilrs reports stick up as positive Y, unlike the browser's axis.
                up: axis_y > GAMEPAD_DEADZONE || pad.is_pressed(Button::DPadUp),
                down: axis_y < -GAMEPAD_DEADZONE || pad.is_pressed(Button::DPadDown),
                jump: pad.is_pressed(Button::South),
                attack: pad.is_pressed(Button::West),
                back: pad.is_pressed(Button::East),
                pause: pad.is_pressed(Button::Start),
                dash: pad.is_pressed(Button::LeftTrigger)
                    || pad.is_pressed(Button::RightTrigger),
            };
        }
    }

    fn pad_pressed(&self, slot: usize, get: fn(&PadState) -> bool) -> bool {
        get(&self.cur[slot]) && !get(&self.prev[slot])
    }

    pub fn read(&mut self, player_schemes: &[usize]) -> InputFrame {
        self.poll_pads();

        let mut frame = InputFrame {
            pads_connected: self.connected,
            ..Default::default()
        };

        // Menus accept any keyboard and any pad, so a player can always navigate
        // regardless of what they picked in settings.
        frame.menu = MenuIntent {
            up: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Up) || is_key_pressed(KeyCode::W),
            down: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Down) || is_key_pressed(KeyCode::S),
            left: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Left) || is_key_pressed(KeyCode::A),
            right: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Right) || is_key_pressed(KeyCode::D),
            confirm: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Enter) || is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Space),
            back: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Escape),
        };
        frame.pause = is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Escape);
        // Nothing a player reaches for, and the core only acts on it from the
        // title screen.
        frame.dev_menu = is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Key8);

        for slot in 0..MAX_PLAYERS {
            if !self.connected[slot] {
                continue;
            }
            frame.menu.up |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.up);
            frame.menu.down |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.down);
            frame.menu.left |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.left);
            frame.menu.right |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.right);
            frame.menu.confirm |=
                self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.jump) || self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.attack);
            frame.menu.back |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.back);
            frame.pause |= self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.pause);
        }

        for (player_index, &scheme_index) in player_schemes.iter().enumerate().take(MAX_PLAYERS) {
            let scheme = &SCHEMES[scheme_index.min(SCHEMES.len() - 1)];
            frame.players[player_index] = match scheme.is_gamepad {
                false => {
                    if scheme_index == SCHEME_KB_WASD {
                        PlayerIntent {
                            left: is_key_down(KeyCode::A),
                            right: is_key_down(KeyCode::D),
                            jump: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::W),
                            slam: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::S),
                            attack: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Space),
                            attack_held: is_key_down(KeyCode::Space),
                            dash: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::LeftShift),
                        }
                    } else {
                        PlayerIntent {
                            left: is_key_down(KeyCode::Left),
                            right: is_key_down(KeyCode::Right),
                            jump: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Up),
                            slam: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Down),
                            attack: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::Enter),
                            attack_held: is_key_down(KeyCode::Enter),
                            // The second keyboard player gets the other Shift,
                            // which is the only key on that side of the board
                            // that pairs with the arrows the way LeftShift does
                            // with WASD.
                            dash: is_key_pressed(KeyCode::RightShift),
                        }
                    }
                }
                true => {
                    let slot = scheme.pad_index;
                    if slot >= MAX_PLAYERS || !self.connected[slot] {
                        PlayerIntent::default()
                    } else {
                        PlayerIntent {
                            left: self.cur[slot].left,
                            right: self.cur[slot].right,
                            jump: self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.jump),
                            attack: self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.attack),
                            attack_held: self.cur[slot].attack,
                            // Slam is B or d-pad down, whichever the player reaches for.
                            slam: self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.back)
                                || self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.down),
                            dash: self.pad_pressed(slot, |s| s.dash),
                        }
                    }
                }
            };
        }

        frame
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: erect-desktop/src/main.rs
================================================================================

// A release build on Windows should not drag a console window along behind it.
// Debug builds keep it: that is where the "sound disabled" note goes.
#![cfg_attr(
    all(target_os = "windows", not(debug_assertions)),
    windows_subsystem = "windows"
)]

//! E-Rect - Rust port of the original canvas game.
//!
//! The browser version stepped its simulation once per rendered frame, so the
//! whole game ran at double speed on a 120 Hz display. Here the loop feeds a
//! fixed-rate accumulator instead: every tuning constant keeps the meaning it
//! had (they are all per-tick), but speed no longer depends on the monitor.
//!
//! One binary serves three shapes of machine. A desktop fills its window and
//! reads a keyboard; a browser gets the phone layout, a letterboxed field with
//! a pad drawn under it, and no sound at all - the mixer wants raw device
//! access, which is the one thing a first browser build does not have. Silence
//! was already survivable: the game has always treated sound as optional.

mod input;
mod persist;
mod render;
#[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
mod sound;
mod touch;
#[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
mod sound_web;
#[cfg(all(feature = "harness", not(target_arch = "wasm32")))]
mod harness;

use erect_core::config::TICK_SECONDS;
use erect_core::game::Game;
use erect_core::geom::Viewport;
use erect_core::input::InputFrame;
use input::InputReader;
use macroquad::prelude::*;
use render::Renderer;

fn window_conf() -> Conf {
    Conf {
        window_title: "E-Rect".to_owned(),
        window_width: 1024,
        window_height: 640,
        high_dpi: true,
        ..Default::default()
    }
}

/// Folds a second source of intents into the first. Nothing is exclusive: a
/// browser build on a laptop can be played with the keyboard and the drawn pad
/// at the same time, and neither has to know about the other.
fn merge(into: &mut InputFrame, from: &InputFrame) {
    for (dst, src) in into.players.iter_mut().zip(from.players.iter()) {
        dst.left |= src.left;
        dst.right |= src.right;
        dst.jump |= src.jump;
        dst.slam |= src.slam;
        dst.attack |= src.attack;
        dst.attack_held |= src.attack_held;
        dst.dash |= src.dash;
    }
    into.menu.up |= from.menu.up;
    into.menu.down |= from.menu.down;
    into.menu.left |= from.menu.left;
    into.menu.right |= from.menu.right;
    into.menu.confirm |= from.menu.confirm;
    into.menu.back |= from.menu.back;
    into.pause |= from.pause;
    into.dev_menu |= from.dev_menu;
}

/// The one-shot half of a frame, with the held half dropped.
///
/// Held state is re-read every frame and must not be carried; edges are the
/// opposite - they happen once and have to survive until a tick can take them.
fn edges_only(frame: &InputFrame) -> InputFrame {
    let mut kept = *frame;
    for p in kept.players.iter_mut() {
        p.left = false;
        p.right = false;
        p.attack_held = false;
    }
    kept
}

/// Anything at all from the player, for the one moment the game needs to know
/// only that something happened.
#[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
fn touched() -> bool {
    !touches().is_empty()
        || is_mouse_button_pressed(MouseButton::Left)
        || get_last_key_pressed().is_some()
}

#[macroquad::main(window_conf)]
async fn main() {
    // Embedded so the binary is self-contained on every platform - no asset
    // directory to ship alongside it, which is also what makes it work from a
    // browser with nothing but the wasm file.
    let font = load_ttf_font_from_bytes(include_bytes!("../assets/font.ttf"))
        .expect("bundled font failed to load");

    let mut viewport = Viewport::new(screen_width(), screen_height());
    let mut settings = persist::load();
    // The launch counter is what guarantees a different run even where the
    // clock does not move; the PSP needs it, and it costs nothing here.
    settings.launches = settings.launches.wrapping_add(1);
    settings.dirty = true;
    let seed = (get_time() * 1_000_000.0) as u64
        ^ 0x5DEECE66D
        ^ settings.launches.rotate_left(42);
    let mut game = Game::new(viewport, seed, settings, &input::SCHEMES, 2);

    // Sound is optional: a missing pack or no output device must not stop play.
    #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
    let sound = match sound::Sound::start(&sound::packs_dir(), seed) {
        Ok(s) => Some(s),
        Err(why) => {
            eprintln!("sound disabled: {why}");
            None
        }
    };

    let renderer = Renderer::new(font);
    let mut reader = InputReader::new();
    let mut pad = touch::TouchPad::new(screen_width(), screen_height());
    // A browser is assumed to be a phone until proven otherwise - and even on a
    // desktop browser the pad is what wants testing. Native builds start on the
    // full window and can switch with F2 to check the layout with a mouse.
    let mut phone_layout = cfg!(target_arch = "wasm32");

    // Unattended run, if one was asked for. Set up after the game exists and
    // before the first frame, so the dev parameters are in place by frame zero.
    #[cfg(all(feature = "harness", not(target_arch = "wasm32")))]
    let mut harness = harness::Harness::from_env(&mut game);
    #[cfg(all(feature = "harness", not(target_arch = "wasm32")))]
    let mut harness_frame = 0u32;

    // Fetch the soundtrack before the first frame of play. Nine files off the
    // network, a screen between each so the page is never just still.
    #[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
    let mut sound = {
        // Wait to be touched before asking for a note. A browser keeps its
        // audio suspended until then, and a suspended context never finishes
        // decoding - so this is not a courtesy, it is the only order in which
        // the loading below can make progress at all.
        while !touched() {
            renderer.render_press_to_start(screen_width(), screen_height());
            next_frame().await;
        }

        let mut loader = sound_web::Loader::new(seed);
        while !loader.settled() {
            renderer.render_loading(
                screen_width(),
                screen_height(),
                loader.done(),
                loader.total(),
                loader.current(),
            );
            next_frame().await;
            loader.pump().await;
        }
        let why = loader.problem().map(|w| w.to_string());
        let count = loader.loaded();
        let sound = loader.finish();
        // Say so and carry on. Silence is a worse game, not a broken one - but
        // it should never be a silence nobody explained.
        if sound.is_none() {
            let why = why.unwrap_or_else(|| format!("only {count} files arrived"));
            for _ in 0..180 {
                renderer.render_sound_failed(screen_width(), screen_height(), &why);
                next_frame().await;
            }
        }
        sound
    };

    let mut accumulator = 0.0f64;
    let mut last = get_time();
    // Presses no tick has taken yet. A rendered frame does not always advance
    // the simulation: the browser draws at its own rate and the game steps at a
    // fixed 60, so some frames produce no tick at all. An edge landing on one of
    // those used to be thrown away, which is why roughly every third attack went
    // missing while the button visibly lit up.
    let mut pending = InputFrame::default();

    loop {
        let (win_w, win_h) = (screen_width(), screen_height());
        if is_key_pressed(KeyCode::F2) {
            phone_layout = !phone_layout;
        }
        pad.resize(win_w, win_h);
        let field = if phone_layout {
            pad.layout().field
        } else {
            Rect::new(0.0, 0.0, win_w, win_h)
        };

        viewport.sync(field.w, field.h);
        game.viewport = viewport;
        game.gravity = viewport.hper(1.0);

        let now = get_time();
        let mut dt = now - last;
        last = now;
        // Clamp so a stall (window drag, breakpoint, a backgrounded tab) cannot
        // spiral into a long catch-up burst of ticks.
        if dt > 0.25 {
            dt = 0.25;
        }
        accumulator += dt;

        let mut frame = reader.read(&game.player_schemes());
        if phone_layout {
            let touched = pad.read();
            merge(&mut frame, &touched);
        }
        // Anything the last frame raised but no tick consumed.
        merge(&mut frame, &pending);
        let pads = frame.pads_connected;

        let mut ticked = false;
        while accumulator >= TICK_SECONDS {
            #[cfg(all(feature = "harness", not(target_arch = "wasm32")))]
            if let Some(h) = harness.as_ref() {
                h.before_tick(&mut game);
                h.drive(&mut frame, &game, harness_frame);
            }
            game.tick(&frame);
            accumulator -= TICK_SECONDS;
            if !ticked {
                // One-shot intents belong to exactly one tick.
                frame.clear_edges();
                ticked = true;
            }
        }

        // Either a tick took the edges, or they wait for the next frame.
        pending = if ticked {
            InputFrame::default()
        } else {
            edges_only(&frame)
        };

        #[cfg(not(target_arch = "wasm32"))]
        if let Some(s) = &sound {
            s.set_roll(game.audio_roll);
            s.set_volumes(game.settings.music_volume, game.settings.sfx_volume);
            s.set_state(game.music_state());
            for event in game.audio.drain() {
                s.fire(event);
            }
        } else {
            game.audio.clear();
        }
        #[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]
        if let Some(s) = sound.as_mut() {
            s.set_volumes(game.settings.music_volume, game.settings.sfx_volume);
            s.update(game.music_state(), (dt * 1000.0) as f32);
            for event in game.audio.drain() {
